{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011MNWRmcewwSTPge1HbZKLn","type":"tool_result","content":"2380\t    try:\n2381\t        from aria_kernel.budget import (\n2382\t            PRICING_SOURCE_FAMILY,\n2383\t            price_tokens,\n2384\t            record_cost_attribution,\n2385\t        )\n2386\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-476 — price_tokens, not estimate_tokens_usd: the bare\n2387\t        # float discards HOW the price was derived, so a family estimate would\n2388\t        # be filed as though it were a measured rate.\n2389\t        priced = price_tokens(\n2390\t            model=model, input_tokens=input_tokens, output_tokens=output_tokens,\n2391\t        )\n2392\t        estimated_usd = actual_cost_usd if actual_cost_usd is not None else priced.usd\n2393\t        if (\n2394\t            actual_cost_usd is None\n2395\t            and priced.source == PRICING_SOURCE_FAMILY\n2396\t            and (input_tokens or output_tokens)\n2397\t        ):\n2398\t            # A new model generation the exact table has not caught up with.\n2399\t            # The charge is real enough to keep the caps binding, but the\n2400\t            # operator needs to know it is inferred so the rate can be\n2401\t            # corrected — silence here is how an estimate becomes \"the number\".\n2402\t            try:\n2403\t                from aria_kernel.tool_registry import append_tools_governance\n2404\t                append_tools_governance(\n2405\t                    tools_dir,\n2406\t                    \"cost_pricing_inferred_from_family\",\n2407\t                    {\n2408\t                        \"model\": model,\n2409\t                        \"matched_family\": priced.matched_key,\n2410\t                        \"estimated_usd\": priced.usd,\n2411\t                        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2412\t                    },\n2413\t                )\n2414\t            except Exception:\n2415\t                pass\n2416\t        if estimated_usd == 0.0 and (input_tokens or output_tokens):\n2417\t            # Tokens were consumed but no price resolved — make the zero\n2418\t            # loud instead of silently under-counting the caps.\n2419\t            try:\n2420\t                from aria_kernel.tool_registry import append_tools_governance\n2421\t                append_tools_governance(\n2422\t                    tools_dir,\n2423\t                    \"cost_pricing_unknown_model\",\n2424\t                    {\n2425\t                        \"model\": model,\n2426\t                        \"input_tokens\": input_tokens,\n2427\t                        \"output_tokens\": output_tokens,\n2428\t                        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2429\t                    },\n2430\t                )\n2431\t            except Exception:\n2432\t                pass\n2433\t        record_cost_attribution(\n2434\t            cycle_id=cycle_id,\n2435\t            plan_id=plan_id,\n2436\t            agent_role=role,\n2437\t            model=model,\n2438\t            input_tokens=input_tokens,\n2439\t            output_tokens=output_tokens,\n2440\t            estimated_usd=estimated_usd,\n2441\t            pressure_source_type=pressure_source_type,\n2442\t            signer_key_fp=signer_key_fp,\n2443\t            base_dir=tools_dir,\n2444\t        )\n2445\t    except Exception:\n2446\t        return\n2447\t\n2448\t\n2449\t# Regex tuned for ```json ... ``` fenced blocks anywhere in the agent\n2450\t# text. Re-used by _extract_envelope_json to find the envelope payload.\n2451\t_FENCED_JSON_RE = re.compile(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*(\\{.*?\\})\\s*```\", re.DOTALL)\n2452\t\n2453\t\n2454\tdef _extract_envelope_json(text: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n2455\t    \"\"\"Find the agent's embedded envelope JSON in a natural-language reply.\n2456\t\n2457\t    Scan order (first match wins):\n2458\t      1. Fenced ```json``` blocks containing a JSON object.\n2459\t      2. Last balanced top-level {...} block in the text.\n2460\t\n2461\t    Returns the parsed dict or None when no JSON is recoverable.\n2462\t    \"\"\"\n2463\t    # Pass 1: fenced JSON blocks — prefer the LAST one (agents typically\n2464\t    # narrate first then close with the envelope).\n2465\t    matches = _FENCED_JSON_RE.findall(text)\n2466\t    for body in reversed(matches):\n2467\t        try:\n2468\t            data = json.loads(body)\n2469\t        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n2470\t            continue\n2471\t        if isinstance(data, dict):\n2472\t            return data\n2473\t    # Pass 2: balanced-brace scan from end-of-text for the last {...} block.\n2474\t    depth = 0\n2475\t    end_idx = -1\n2476\t    for i in range(len(text) - 1, -1, -1):\n2477\t        ch = text[i]\n2478\t        if ch == \"}\":\n2479\t            if depth == 0:\n2480\t                end_idx = i\n2481\t            depth += 1\n2482\t        elif ch == \"{\":\n2483\t            depth -= 1\n2484\t            if depth == 0 and end_idx > 0:\n2485\t                candidate = text[i : end_idx + 1]\n2486\t                try:\n2487\t                    data = json.loads(candidate)\n2488\t                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n2489\t                    end_idx = -1\n2490\t                    continue\n2491\t                if isinstance(data, dict):\n2492\t                    return data\n2493\t    return None\n2494\t\n2495\t\n2496\tdef _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2497\t    *,\n2498\t    raw_stdout: str,\n2499\t    request_id: str,\n2500\t    claim_id: str,\n2501\t    agent_id: str,\n2502\t    role: str,\n2503\t    subagent_type: str,\n2504\t    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n2505\t    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n2506\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n2507\t    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n2508\t\n2509\t    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n2510\t    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n2511\t    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n2512\t    \"\"\"\n2513\t    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n2514\t    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n2515\t    if not agent_text:\n2516\t        agent_text = raw_stdout\n2517\t    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n2518\t\n2519\t    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n2520\t        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n2521\t        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2522\t        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n2523\t        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n2524\t        \"role\": role,\n2525\t        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n2526\t    }\n2527\t\n2528\t    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n2529\t    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n2530\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n2531\t    else:\n2532\t        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n2533\t        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n2534\t        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n2535\t        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n2536\t        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n2537\t        # the reply.\n2538\t        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n2539\t        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n2540\t        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n2541\t        if must_satisfy:\n2542\t            for item in must_satisfy:\n2543\t                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n2544\t                    continue\n2545\t                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n2546\t                if not cid:\n2547\t                    continue\n2548\t                synthesized.append({\n2549\t                    \"id\": cid,\n2550\t                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2551\t                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2552\t                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2553\t                })\n2554\t        if not synthesized:\n2555\t            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n2556\t            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n2557\t            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n2558\t            synthesized.append({\n2559\t                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n2560\t                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2561\t                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2562\t                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2563\t            })\n2564\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n2565\t\n2566\t    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n2567\t    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n2568\t        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n2569\t            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n2570\t    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n2571\t        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n2572\t\n2573\t    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n2574\t    # forensic context post-submission.\n2575\t    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n2576\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n2577\t        details = {}\n2578\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n2579\t    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n2580\t    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n2581\t    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n2582\t    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n2583\t    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n2584\t    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n2585\t    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n2586\t    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n2587\t    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n2588\t    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n2589\t    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n2590\t    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n2591\t    if dispatch_model:\n2592\t        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n2593\t    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n2594\t    usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n2595\t    if usage is not None:\n2596\t        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n2597\t    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n2598\t\n2599\t    return envelope\n2600\t\n2601\t\n2602\tdef _rejected_result_recorded(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n2603\t    \"\"\"True when the submit step's answer is a kernel-recorded REJECTED result row.\n2604\t\n2605\t    `aria_kernel agent submit-result` prints its result document on stdout and\n2606\t    exits 1 for anything but `accepted`. When that document carries a result\n2607\t    row with status `rejected`, the kernel has already appended the row: the\n2608\t    claim is terminal (`_assert_lifecycle_mutation_allowed`) and the request\n2609\t    derives REJECTED (`derive_request_state`, results dominate). Anything else\n2610\t    on a non-zero exit is a refusal before the row and leaves the claim live.\n2611\t    \"\"\"\n2612\t    text = submit_stdout or \"\"\n2613\t    start = text.find(\"{\")\n2614\t    if start < 0:\n2615\t        return False\n2616\t    try:\n2617\t        payload = json.loads(text[start:])\n2618\t    except ValueError:\n2619\t        return False\n2620\t    row = payload.get(\"row\") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None\n2621\t    return (isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get(\"status\") == \"rejected\"\n2622\t            and isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"row_type\") == \"result\" and row.get(\"status\") == \"rejected\")\n2623\t\n2624\t\n2625\tdef _held_request_state(request_id: str, base_dir: Path) -> str | None:\n2626\t    \"\"\"The lease guard's question to the kernel: what state is the request in?\n2627\t\n2628\t    None when the kernel has no such request — nothing can be held, so the\n2629\t    guard has nothing to hand back (the mocked executor fixtures claim a\n2630\t    request that was never minted). `derive_request_state` refuses an\n2631\t    unknown id, and a refusal the guard could not tell from an unreadable\n2632\t    ledger would make it release on every mocked run.\n2633\t    \"\"\"\n2634\t    from aria_kernel.agent_invocations import list_agent_invocation_requests\n2635\t\n2636\t    if not list_agent_invocation_requests(request_id=request_id, base_dir=base_dir):\n2637\t        return None\n2638\t    return _derive_request_state(request_id=request_id, base_dir=base_dir)\n2639\t\n2640\t\n2641\tdef _rejected_only_for_verification_unavailable(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n2642\t    \"\"\"Did the kernel reject the submission SOLELY because it could not verify?\n2643\t\n2644\t    The kernel CLI prints its verdict as JSON on stdout: on a rejection,\n2645\t    ``reasons`` (prose, for the ledger and the operator) and\n2646\t    ``rejection_codes`` (one machine code per reason). This reads the codes\n2647\t    — never the prose — and answers True only when every code says the\n2648\t    kernel's evidence probe could not run (git did not answer inside its\n2649\t    bound on a loaded host). That is the harness's gap, not the work's: the\n2650\t    same envelope resubmitted on a quiet host usually verifies, and it must\n2651\t    not burn the request's requeue budget the way `submit_rejected` does\n2652\t    (the 2026-08-09 `baseline_unavailable` class, at the acceptance seam).\n2653\t\n2654\t    Anything else — unparseable output, a kernel exception, no codes, or\n2655\t    ONE code that is about the work — is the request's fault: fail toward\n2656\t    the human, never toward silent infinite retry.\n2657\t    \"\"\"\n2658\t    try:\n2659\t        payload = json.loads(submit_stdout or \"\")\n2660\t    except ValueError:\n2661\t        return False\n2662\t    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or payload.get(\"status\") != \"rejected\":\n2663\t        return False\n2664\t    codes = payload.get(\"rejection_codes\")\n2665\t    if not isinstance(codes, list) or not codes:\n2666\t        return False\n2667\t    return all(\n2668\t        isinstance(code, str) and code in _EVIDENCE_VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE_CODES\n2669\t        for code in codes\n2670\t    )\n2671\t\n2672\t\n2673\tdef _release_claim(\n2674\t    *,\n2675\t    tools_dir: Path,\n2676\t    repo: Path,\n2677\t    claim_id: str,\n2678\t    agent_id: str,\n2679\t    lease_token: str,"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"fa867ea0-1256-40ce-9253-26492cb57481","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:34:05.813Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"    try:\n        from aria_kernel.budget import (\n            PRICING_SOURCE_FAMILY,\n            price_tokens,\n            record_cost_attribution,\n        )\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-476 — price_tokens, not estimate_tokens_usd: the bare\n        # float discards HOW the price was derived, so a family estimate would\n        # be filed as though it were a measured rate.\n        priced = price_tokens(\n            model=model, input_tokens=input_tokens, output_tokens=output_tokens,\n        )\n        estimated_usd = actual_cost_usd if actual_cost_usd is not None else priced.usd\n        if (\n            actual_cost_usd is None\n            and priced.source == PRICING_SOURCE_FAMILY\n            and (input_tokens or output_tokens)\n        ):\n            # A new model generation the exact table has not caught up with.\n            # The charge is real enough to keep the caps binding, but the\n            # operator needs to know it is inferred so the rate can be\n            # corrected — silence here is how an estimate becomes \"the number\".\n            try:\n                from aria_kernel.tool_registry import append_tools_governance\n                append_tools_governance(\n                    tools_dir,\n                    \"cost_pricing_inferred_from_family\",\n                    {\n                        \"model\": model,\n                        \"matched_family\": priced.matched_key,\n                        \"estimated_usd\": priced.usd,\n                        \"request_id\": request_id,\n                    },\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n        if estimated_usd == 0.0 and (input_tokens or output_tokens):\n            # Tokens were consumed but no price resolved — make the zero\n            # loud instead of silently under-counting the caps.\n            try:\n                from aria_kernel.tool_registry import append_tools_governance\n                append_tools_governance(\n                    tools_dir,\n                    \"cost_pricing_unknown_model\",\n                    {\n                        \"model\": model,\n                        \"input_tokens\": input_tokens,\n                        \"output_tokens\": output_tokens,\n                        \"request_id\": request_id,\n                    },\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n        record_cost_attribution(\n            cycle_id=cycle_id,\n            plan_id=plan_id,\n            agent_role=role,\n            model=model,\n            input_tokens=input_tokens,\n            output_tokens=output_tokens,\n            estimated_usd=estimated_usd,\n            pressure_source_type=pressure_source_type,\n            signer_key_fp=signer_key_fp,\n            base_dir=tools_dir,\n        )\n    except Exception:\n        return\n\n\n# Regex tuned for ```json ... ``` fenced blocks anywhere in the agent\n# text. Re-used by _extract_envelope_json to find the envelope payload.\n_FENCED_JSON_RE = re.compile(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*(\\{.*?\\})\\s*```\", re.DOTALL)\n\n\ndef _extract_envelope_json(text: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Find the agent's embedded envelope JSON in a natural-language reply.\n\n    Scan order (first match wins):\n      1. Fenced ```json``` blocks containing a JSON object.\n      2. Last balanced top-level {...} block in the text.\n\n    Returns the parsed dict or None when no JSON is recoverable.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pass 1: fenced JSON blocks — prefer the LAST one (agents typically\n    # narrate first then close with the envelope).\n    matches = _FENCED_JSON_RE.findall(text)\n    for body in reversed(matches):\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(body)\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n            continue\n        if isinstance(data, dict):\n            return data\n    # Pass 2: balanced-brace scan from end-of-text for the last {...} block.\n    depth = 0\n    end_idx = -1\n    for i in range(len(text) - 1, -1, -1):\n        ch = text[i]\n        if ch == \"}\":\n            if depth == 0:\n                end_idx = i\n            depth += 1\n        elif ch == \"{\":\n            depth -= 1\n            if depth == 0 and end_idx > 0:\n                candidate = text[i : end_idx + 1]\n                try:\n                    data = json.loads(candidate)\n                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                    end_idx = -1\n                    continue\n                if isinstance(data, dict):\n                    return data\n    return None\n\n\ndef _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n    *,\n    raw_stdout: str,\n    request_id: str,\n    claim_id: str,\n    agent_id: str,\n    role: str,\n    subagent_type: str,\n    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n\n    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n    \"\"\"\n    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n    if not agent_text:\n        agent_text = raw_stdout\n    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n\n    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n        \"request_id\": request_id,\n        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n        \"role\": role,\n        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n    }\n\n    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n    else:\n        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n        # the reply.\n        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n        if must_satisfy:\n            for item in must_satisfy:\n                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n                    continue\n                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n                if not cid:\n                    continue\n                synthesized.append({\n                    \"id\": cid,\n                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n                })\n        if not synthesized:\n            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n            synthesized.append({\n                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n            })\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n\n    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n\n    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n    # forensic context post-submission.\n    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n    if dispatch_model:\n        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n    usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n    if usage is not None:\n        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n\n    return envelope\n\n\ndef _rejected_result_recorded(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when the submit step's answer is a kernel-recorded REJECTED result row.\n\n    `aria_kernel agent submit-result` prints its result document on stdout and\n    exits 1 for anything but `accepted`. When that document carries a result\n    row with status `rejected`, the kernel has already appended the row: the\n    claim is terminal (`_assert_lifecycle_mutation_allowed`) and the request\n    derives REJECTED (`derive_request_state`, results dominate). Anything else\n    on a non-zero exit is a refusal before the row and leaves the claim live.\n    \"\"\"\n    text = submit_stdout or \"\"\n    start = text.find(\"{\")\n    if start < 0:\n        return False\n    try:\n        payload = json.loads(text[start:])\n    except ValueError:\n        return False\n    row = payload.get(\"row\") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None\n    return (isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get(\"status\") == \"rejected\"\n            and isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"row_type\") == \"result\" and row.get(\"status\") == \"rejected\")\n\n\ndef _held_request_state(request_id: str, base_dir: Path) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"The lease guard's question to the kernel: what state is the request in?\n\n    None when the kernel has no such request — nothing can be held, so the\n    guard has nothing to hand back (the mocked executor fixtures claim a\n    request that was never minted). `derive_request_state` refuses an\n    unknown id, and a refusal the guard could not tell from an unreadable\n    ledger would make it release on every mocked run.\n    \"\"\"\n    from aria_kernel.agent_invocations import list_agent_invocation_requests\n\n    if not list_agent_invocation_requests(request_id=request_id, base_dir=base_dir):\n        return None\n    return _derive_request_state(request_id=request_id, base_dir=base_dir)\n\n\ndef _rejected_only_for_verification_unavailable(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Did the kernel reject the submission SOLELY because it could not verify?\n\n    The kernel CLI prints its verdict as JSON on stdout: on a rejection,\n    ``reasons`` (prose, for the ledger and the operator) and\n    ``rejection_codes`` (one machine code per reason). This reads the codes\n    — never the prose — and answers True only when every code says the\n    kernel's evidence probe could not run (git did not answer inside its\n    bound on a loaded host). That is the harness's gap, not the work's: the\n    same envelope resubmitted on a quiet host usually verifies, and it must\n    not burn the request's requeue budget the way `submit_rejected` does\n    (the 2026-08-09 `baseline_unavailable` class, at the acceptance seam).\n\n    Anything else — unparseable output, a kernel exception, no codes, or\n    ONE code that is about the work — is the request's fault: fail toward\n    the human, never toward silent infinite retry.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        payload = json.loads(submit_stdout or \"\")\n    except ValueError:\n        return False\n    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or payload.get(\"status\") != \"rejected\":\n        return False\n    codes = payload.get(\"rejection_codes\")\n    if not isinstance(codes, list) or not codes:\n        return False\n    return all(\n        isinstance(code, str) and code in _EVIDENCE_VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE_CODES\n        for code in codes\n    )\n\n\ndef _release_claim(\n    *,\n    tools_dir: Path,\n    repo: Path,\n    claim_id: str,\n    agent_id: str,\n    lease_token: str,","numLines":300,"startLine":2380,"totalLines":5265}}}

Stale docs are worse\n6\t// than missing docs: they answer confidently and wrongly.\n7\t// WHAT (deterministic, fs-only): every backtick-quoted repo path in\n8\t// docs/**/*.md is resolved against the working tree; a reference to a path\n9\t// that no longer exists is a `doc_references_missing_path` finding with the\n10\t// doc's file:line as evidence. Globs, placeholders, and line-suffixed refs\n11\t// are handled so the rule stays low-noise.\n12\timport { relative } from 'node:path';\n13\t\n14\timport {\n15\t  collectFiles,\n16\t  filterFilesBySnapshot,\n17\t  normalizeWorkspacePath,\n18\t  readWorkspaceFile,\n19\t  requireScanRoots,\n20\t  resolveInsideWorkspace,\n21\t  workspacePathExists,\n22\t} from './adapter-fs';\n23\t\n24\tinterface AdapterInput {\n25\t  readonly roots?: readonly string[];\n26\t  readonly repo_snapshot?: { readonly allowed_paths?: readonly string[] };\n27\t}\n28\t\n29\tinterface EvidenceRef {\n30\t  readonly path: string;\n31\t  readonly line?: number;\n32\t}\n33\t\n34\tinterface AdapterObservation {\n35\t  readonly id: string;\n36\t  readonly type: string;\n37\t  readonly path?: string;\n38\t  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\tinterface AdapterFinding {\n42\t  readonly id: string;\n43\t  readonly rule: `feature/branch-name` or `owner/repo` never\n64\t// matches, which is what keeps this rule quiet.\n65\tconst PATH_PREFIXES = [\n66\t  'apps/',\n67\t  'e2e/',\n68\t  'infrastructure/',\n69\t  'libs/',\n70\t  'platform/',\n71\t  'scripts/',\n72\t  'sens-api-gateway/',\n73\t  'tools/',\n74\t  'web/',\n75\t];\n76\t\n77\tconst BACKTICK_SPAN_RE = /`([^`\\n]+)`/g;\n78\t\n79\tfunction candidateRef(span: string): string | undefined {\n80\t  let text = span.trim();\n81\t  // `path:123` / `path:12-40` — evidence-style refs pin a line, the claim\n82\t  // is about the path.\n83\t  text = text.replace(/:[0-9][0-9-]*$/, '');\n84\t  if (!PATH_PREFIXES.some((prefix) => text.startsWith(prefix))) {\n85\t    return undefined;\n86\t  }\n87\t  // Globs and placeholders are patterns, not path claims.\n88\t  if (/[*{}<>$\\s]/.test(text) || text.includes('...')) {\n89\t    return undefined;\n90\t  }\n91\t  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_@./-]+$/.test(text)) {\n92\t    return undefined;\n93\t  }\n94\t  return text.replace(/\\/$/, '');\n95\t}\n96\t\n97\texport function analyzeDocStaleness(\n98\t  input: AdapterInput,\n99\t  workspaceRoot = process.cwd(),\n100\t): AriaOutput {\n101\t  const roots = requireScanRoots('doc-staleness-adapter', input.roots);\n102\t  const observations: AdapterObservation[] = [];\n103\t  const findings: AdapterFinding[] = [];\n104\t  const readPaths: string[] = [];\n105\t\n106\t  const docs = filterFilesBySnapshot(\n107\t    roots\n108\t      .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n109\t      .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n110\t      .flatMap((root) => collectFiles(root, { extensions: ['.md'] })),\n111\t    workspaceRoot,\n112\t    input,\n113\t  );\n114\t\n115\t  let refsChecked = 0;\n116\t  for (const doc of docs) {\n117\t    const docRel = normalizeWorkspacePath(relative(workspaceRoot, doc));\n118\t    readPaths.push(docRel);\n119\t    const lines = readWorkspaceFile(doc).split('\\n');\n120\t    let missingInDoc = 0;\n121\t    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {\n122\t      for (const match of lines[index].matchAll(BACKTICK_SPAN_RE)) {\n123\t        const ref = candidateRef(match[1]);\n124\t        if (ref === undefined) {\n125\t          continue;\n126\t        }\n127\t        refsChecked += 1;\n128\t        if (workspacePathExists(resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, ref))) {\n129\t          continue;\n130\t        }\n131\t        missingInDoc += 1;\n132\t        findings.push({\n133\t          id: `doc-staleness:missing:${docRel}:${index + 1}:${ref}`,\n134\t          rule: 'doc_references_missing_path',\n135\t          severity: 'medium',\n136\t          path: docRel,\n137\t          line: index + 1,\n138\t          message:\n139\t            `\\`${docRel}\\` references \\`${ref}\\`, which no longer exists — ` +\n140\t            'the doc answers confidently about a surface that is gone.',\n141\t          evidence: [{ path: docRel, line: index + 1 }],\n142\t          confidence: 0.85,\n143\t        });\n144\t      }\n145\t    }\n146\t    observations.push({\n147\t      id: `doc-staleness:doc:${docRel}`,\n148\t      type: 'doc_staleness_document',\n149\t      path: docRel,\n150\t      details: { missingRefs: missingInDoc },\n151\t    });\n152\t  }\n153\t\n154\t  const sortedReadPaths = [...new Set(readPaths)].sort();\n155\t  return {\n156\t    observations: observations.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id)),\n157\t    findings: findings.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id)),\n158\t    read_paths: sortedReadPaths,\n159\t    evidence_sources: sortedReadPaths,\n160\t    belief_candidates: [],\n161\t    cost_units: sortedReadPaths.length,\n162\t    metadata: { scanMode: 'doc_staleness_v1', docCount: docs.length, refsChecked },\n163\t  };\n164\t}\n165\t\n166\tfunction readStdin(): Promise<string> {\n167\t  return new Promise((resolvePromise, reject) => {\n168\t    let input = '';\n169\t    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');\n170\t    // setEncoding('utf8') makes every chunk a string at runtime, but the\n171\t    // stream's declared chunk type stays `string | Buffer` — concatenating the\n172\t    // union is what the type checker rejects. Narrow at the boundary rather\n173\t    // than widening `input` (kernel-dead-wire-adapter is the converged shape).\n174\t    process.stdin.on('data', (chunk: string | Buffer) => {\n175\t      input += typeof chunk === 'string' ? chunk : chunk.toString('utf8');\n176\t    });\n177\t    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolvePromise(input));\n178\t    process.stdin.on('error', reject);\n179\t  });\n180\t}\n181\t\n182\tasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n183\t  const rawInput = await readStdin();\n184\t  const input = rawInput.trim().length > 0 ? (JSON.parse(rawInput) as AdapterInput) : {};\n185\t  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(analyzeDocStaleness(input))}\\n`);\n186\t}\n187\t\n188\tif (require.main === module) {\n189\t  main().catch((error: unknown) => {\n190\t    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n191\t    process.stderr.write(`${message}\\n`);\n192\t    process.exit(1);\n193\t  });\n194\t}\n195\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"f81a59d6-acc9-4244-bd63-2e53d7192f3c","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:34:47.130Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/tools/aria-adapters/doc-staleness-adapter.ts","content":"#!/usr/bin/env ts-node\n// D5-documentation dimension adapter v1 (Plan \"ARIA Sinir Sistemi\" FAZ 7).\n//\n// WHY: nothing watched documentation truth — a runbook can name a script\n// that was deleted a year ago and stay green forever. rule quiet.\nconst PATH_PREFIXES = [\n  'apps/',\n  'e2e/',\n  'infrastructure/',\n  'libs/',\n  'platform/',\n  'scripts/',\n  'sens-api-gateway/',\n  'tools/',\n  'web/',\n];\n\nconst are patterns, not path claims.\n  if (/[*{}<>$\\s]/.test(text) || text.includes('...')) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_@./-]+$/.test(text)) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  return text.replace(/\\/$/, '');\n}\n\nexport function analyzeDocStaleness(\n  input: AdapterInput,\n  workspaceRoot = process.cwd(),\n): AriaOutput {\n  const roots = requireScanRoots('doc-staleness-adapter', input.roots);\n  const observations: AdapterObservation[] = [];\n  const findings: AdapterFinding[] = [];\n  const readPaths: string[] = [];\n\n  const docs = filterFilesBySnapshot(\n    roots\n      .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n      .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n      .flatMap((root) => collectFiles(root, { extensions: ['.md'] })),\n    workspaceRoot,\n    input,\n  );\n\n  let refsChecked = 0;\n  for (const doc of docs) {\n    const docRel = normalizeWorkspacePath(relative(workspaceRoot, doc));\n    readPaths.push(docRel);\n    const lines = readWorkspaceFile(doc).split('\\n');\n    let missingInDoc = 0;\n    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {\n      for (const match of lines[index].matchAll(BACKTICK_SPAN_RE)) {\n        const ref = candidateRef(match[1]);\n        if (ref === undefined) {\n          continue;\n        }\n        refsChecked += 1;\n        if (workspacePathExists(resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, ref))) {\n          continue;\n        }\n        missingInDoc += 1;\n        findings.push({\n          id: `doc-staleness:missing:${docRel}:${index + 1}:${ref}`,\n          rule: 'doc_references_missing_path',\n          severity: 'medium',\n          path: docRel,\n          line: index + 1,\n          message:\n            `\\`${docRel}\\` references \\`${ref}\\`, which no longer exists — ` +\n            'the doc answers confidently about a surface that is gone.',\n          evidence: [{ path: docRel, line: index + 1 }],\n          confidence: 0.85,\n        });\n      }\n    }\n    observations.push({\n      id: `doc-staleness:doc:${docRel}`,\n      type: 'doc_staleness_document',\n      path: docRel,\n      details: { missingRefs: missingInDoc },\n    });\n  }\n\n  const sortedReadPaths = [...new Set(readPaths)].sort();\n  return {\n    observations: observations.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id)),\n    findings: findings.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id)),\n    read_paths: sortedReadPaths,\n    evidence_sources: sortedReadPaths,\n    belief_candidates: [],\n    cost_units: sortedReadPaths.length,\n    metadata: { scanMode: 'doc_staleness_v1', docCount: docs.length, refsChecked },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction readStdin(): Promise<string> {\n  return new Promise((resolvePromise, reject) => {\n    let input = '';\n    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');\n    // setEncoding('utf8') makes every chunk a string at runtime, but the\n    // stream's declared chunk type stays `string | Buffer` — concatenating the\n    // union is what the type checker rejects. Narrow at the boundary rather\n    // than widening `input` (kernel-dead-wire-adapter is the converged shape).\n    process.stdin.on('data', (chunk: string | Buffer) => {\n      input += typeof chunk === 'string' ? chunk : chunk.toString('utf8');\n    });\n    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolvePromise(input));\n    process.stdin.on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  const rawInput = await readStdin();\n  const input = rawInput.trim().length > 0 ? (JSON.parse(rawInput) as AdapterInput) : {};\n  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(analyzeDocStaleness(input))}\\n`);\n}\n\nif (require.main === module) {\n  main().catch((error: unknown) => {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    process.stderr.write(`${message}\\n`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n}\n","numLines":195,"startLine":1,"totalLines":195}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfEhYU3dt71STPeqNgiB9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01UzkEuLdS1Tv4y7dSbmrjp2","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/agent_eval.py","offset":860,"limit":120},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4122,"cache_read_input_tokens":135131,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4122},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"ec74d397-23c4-4edd-8c92-3634562e8a0b","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:36:01.027Z","request_id":"req_011CfEhYTQga37Ki4MNpCrno","wire_tool_inputs":{"toolu_01UzkEuLdS1Tv4y7dSbmrjp2":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/agent_eval.py","offset":860,"limit":120}}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UzkEuLdS1Tv4y7dSbmrjp2","type":"tool_result","content":"860\t        \"undated_run_count\": undated,\n861\t    }\n862\t\n863\t\n864\tdef count_eval_runs_by_mode(*, base_dir: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n865\t    \"\"\"Plan 020 metric helper — used by plan_016_metrics 11th + 12th counters.\n866\t\n867\t    Returns {'aria_agent_eval_mock_only_total': N, 'aria_agent_eval_real_total': M}.\n868\t    \"\"\"\n869\t    rows = list_eval_runs(base_dir=base_dir)\n870\t    mock = sum(1 for r in rows if r.get(\"mock_mode\") is True)\n871\t    real = sum(1 for r in rows if r.get(\"mock_mode\") is False)\n872\t    return {\n873\t        \"aria_agent_eval_mock_only_total\": mock,\n874\t        \"aria_agent_eval_real_total\": real,\n875\t    }\n876\t\n877\t\n878\t# Plan 022 §H-5 — SHADOW raw findings sampling threshold (24-hour window).\n879\t# When a SHADOW tool produces ≥ this many raw_findings in 24h, the\n880\t# sampling CLI emits a shadow_findings_sampled governance event per\n881\t# tool AND escalates via human_required_recorded so operators see the\n882\t# build-up instead of the raw findings rotting in runs.jsonl.\n883\tSHADOW_SAMPLE_THRESHOLD_24H: int = 5\n884\t\n885\t\n886\tdef sample_shadow_raw_findings(\n887\t    *,\n888\t    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n889\t    threshold_24h: int = SHADOW_SAMPLE_THRESHOLD_24H,\n890\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n891\t    \"\"\"Plan 022 §H-5 — surface SHADOW raw_findings to operator review.\n892\t\n893\t    Pre-Plan-022 SHADOW tools emitted empty operator-facing\n894\t    observations/findings (tool_runner.py:139-141 gates emission on\n895\t    can_emit_operator_facing). raw_findings landed in the ledger but\n896\t    operators never saw them; the dashboard's shadow_raw_delta pressure\n897\t    only triggered when the count was abnormal — base-rate buried.\n898\t\n899\t    Fix: this function reads the last 24h of runs.jsonl, aggregates\n900\t    raw_findings_count per SHADOW tool_id, and:\n901\t    1. Emits a shadow_findings_sampled governance event per tool.\n902\t    2. If the count meets or exceeds threshold_24h, also files a\n903\t       human_required_recorded escalation so the operator dashboard\n904\t       surfaces it as actionable.\n905\t\n906\t    Returns a summary dict {samples: [{tool_id, raw_findings_count_24h,\n907\t    escalated: bool}, ...], escalation_count: int}.\n908\t    \"\"\"\n909\t    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n910\t    from .human_required import record_human_required\n911\t    from .tool_health import runs_path\n912\t    from .runs_reader import read_runs_rows\n913\t    from .tool_registry import (\n914\t        append_tools_governance,\n915\t        ensure_tools_dir,\n916\t        get_tool,\n917\t    )\n918\t\n919\t    enforce_profile_for_write(\"agent_evals\", base_dir=base_dir)\n920\t    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n921\t\n922\t    cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) -        \"undated_run_count\": undated,\n    }\n\n\ndef count_eval_runs_by_mode(*, base_dir: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Plan 020 metric helper — used by plan_016_metrics 11th + 12th counters.\n\n    Returns {'aria_agent_eval_mock_only_total': N, 'aria_agent_eval_real_total': M}.\n    \"\"\"\n    rows = list_eval_runs(base_dir=base_dir)\n    mock = sum(1 for r in rows if r.get(\"mock_mode\") is True)\n    real = sum(1 for r in rows if r.get(\"mock_mode\") is False)\n    return {\n        \"aria_agent_eval_mock_only_total\": mock,\n        \"aria_agent_eval_real_total\": real,\n    }\n\n\n# Plan 022 §H-5 — SHADOW raw findings sampling threshold (24-hour window).\n# When a SHADOW tool produces ≥ this many raw_findings in 24h, the\n# sampling CLI emits a shadow_findings_sampled governance event per\n# tool AND escalates via human_required_recorded so operators see the\n# build-up instead of the raw findings rotting in runs.jsonl.\nSHADOW_SAMPLE_THRESHOLD_24H: int = 5\n\n\ndef sample_shadow_raw_findings(\n    *,\n    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n    threshold_24h: int = SHADOW_SAMPLE_THRESHOLD_24H,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Plan 022 §H-5 — surface SHADOW raw_findings to operator review.\n\n    Pre-Plan-022 SHADOW tools emitted empty operator-facing\n    observations/findings (tool_runner.py:139-141 gates emission on\n    can_emit_operator_facing). raw_findings landed in the ledger but\n    operators never saw them; the dashboard's shadow_raw_delta pressure\n    only triggered when the count was abnormal — base-rate buried.\n\n    Fix: this function reads the last 24h of runs.jsonl, aggregates\n    raw_findings_count per SHADOW tool_id, and:\n    1. Emits a shadow_findings_sampled governance event per tool.\n    2. If the count meets or exceeds threshold_24h, also files a\n       human_required_recorded escalation so the operator dashboard\n       surfaces it as actionable.\n\n    Returns a summary dict {samples: [{tool_id, raw_findings_count_24h,\n    escalated: bool}, ...], escalation_count: int}.\n    \"\"\"\n    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n    from .human_required import record_human_required\n    from .tool_health import runs_path\n    from .runs_reader import read_runs_rows\n    from .tool_registry import (\n        append_tools_governance,\n        ensure_tools_dir,\n        get_tool,\n    )\n\n    enforce_profile_for_write(\"agent_evals\", base_dir=base_dir)\n    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n\n    cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=24)\n    runs = list(read_runs_rows(runs_path(base_dir), base_dir=root))\n    by_tool: dict[str, int] = {}\n    # Plan 023 v3 §C-6 — track runs skipped due to scope_out_mutations\n    # so the sampler output surfaces the suspect-run count separately\n    # from the clean raw_findings aggregate.\n    suspect_by_tool: dict[str, int] = {}\n    for run in runs:\n        recorded = run.get(\"recorded_at\") or run.get(\"at\")\n        if not isinstance(recorded, str):\n            continue\n        try:\n            ts = datetime.fromisoformat(recorded.replace(\"Z\", \"+00:00\"))\n        except ValueError:\n            continue\n        if ts.tzinfo is None:\n            ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n        if ts.astimezone(timezone.utc) < cutoff:\n            continue\n        tool_id = str(run.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\")\n        if not tool_id:\n            continue\n        # Only sample tools currently in SHADOW status — ACTIVE tools\n        # already emit operator-facing findings, so sampling them\n        # would double-surface.\n        try:\n            tool = get_tool(tool_id, base_dir)\n        except GovernanceError:\n            continue\n        if tool.get(\"status\") != \"SHADOW\":\n            continue\n        runner_block = run.get(\"runner\") or {}\n        # Plan 023 v3 §C-6 — skip runs that escaped their declared scope\n        # from the SHADOW raw-findings aggregate. Scope-out mutations\n        # already trigger immediate quarantine via record_run; their\n        # raw findings are flagged invalid_evidence in raw-findings.jsonl\n        # by feedback_store. Surfacing them via the sampler would\n        # re-legitimize a sandbox-escape adapter's output. Track the\n        # suspect_run_count separately so operators see the skip.\n        if runner_block.get(\"scope_out_mutations\"):\n            suspect_by_tool[tool_id] = suspect_by_tool.get(tool_id, 0) + 1\n            continue\n        count = int(runner_block.get(\"raw_findings_count\", 0) or 0)\n        by_tool[tool_id] = by_tool.get(tool_id, 0) + count\n\n    samples: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    escalation_count = 0\n    # Union of tool_ids seen in either clean or suspect path so the\n    # sample list reflects every SHADOW tool with any 24h activity.\n    all_tool_ids = sorted(set(by_tool) | set(suspect_by_tool))\n    for tool_id in all_tool_ids:\n        count = by_tool.get(tool_id, 0)\n        suspect_run_count = suspect_by_tool.get(tool_id, 0)\n        escalated = count >= threshold_24h\n        samples.append({\n            \"tool_id\": tool_id,\n            \"raw_findings_count_24h\": count,\n            \"suspect_run_count_24h\": suspect_run_count,","numLines":120,"startLine":860,"totalLines":1031}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Sv4zWB3jaM4kgyArPCXqgf","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Plan ARIA-V5 §2 V5.2 — Gate B post-implementation adversarial review runner.\n2\t\n3\tV5.2 wires the post-implementation adversarial review loop into the\n4\tautonomy orchestrator. After ``worker_drainer`` completes (workers\n5\thave claimed assignments, verified, and produced implementation\n6\tartifacts), the orchestrator calls ``review_runner`` BEFORE\n7\t``auto_merge_runner`` — gating auto-merge on\n8\t``review_verdict == \"no_gaps\"``.\n9\t\n10\tOperator vision (Plan ARIA-V5 §1, verbatim):\n11\t  \"ımplementerler ımplement ettıkten sonra da eksık varmı yanlıs varmı\n12\t   dıye agentlar yıne kontrol etmelı\"\n13\t\n14\tThe drainer mints adversarial_judge + evidence_judge envelopes\n15\t(post-impl judgment roles already in ``agent_invocations.ROLES``).\n16\tDefault loop:\n17\t\n18\t  1. round 1 — mint adversarial_judgment envelope; poll for\n19\t     submission until ``judge_timeout_seconds`` expires\n20\t  2. if adversarial judge returns gaps → mint evidence_judgment\n21\t     envelope (second opinion); combine verdicts\n22\t  3. if both judges say no_gaps → return ``no_gaps``\n23\t  4. if judges disagree → return ``judge_split``\n24\t  5. loop to next round if gaps_open up to ``max_review_rounds``\n25\t  6. on cap → return ``max_review_rounds``\n26\t  7. if cycle_id ↔ impl_artifacts_ref hash mismatch → return\n27\t     ``artifact_mismatch``\n28\t\n29\tDefensive default: when no real adversarial / evidence judge is\n30\tclaiming envelopes (typical autonomous-run mode without real Claude Code\n31\t/ Claude Code dispatchers), the drainer times out at ``gaps_open``;\n32\tthe orchestrator then skips ``auto_merge_runner`` cleanly. This is\n33\tthe correct fail-closed behaviour — no review consensus, no\n34\tauto-merge. Future V6+ work wires real judges to consume the\n35\tenvelopes.\n36\t\n37\tORPHAN-HIGH-422 evidence invariant:\n38\t  ``no_gaps`` is derived ONLY from an accepted result row bound to the\n39\t  round's own request id AND role, carried back on\n40\t  ``ReviewResult.accepted_result_ref``. Pre-fix the loop inferred success\n41\t  from ``next_pending_request(...) is None`` — but a request leaves the\n42\t  pending set on claim, rejection, cancellation, staleness and\n43\t  HUMAN_REQUIRED escalation, so the one signal meaning \"a human must look\n44\t  at this\" was the signal that cleared the gate. Absence of work waiting\n45\t  is not evidence that work was done.\n46\t\n47\tTests inject mock review runners via the ``review_runner`` kwarg on\n48\t``run_autonomy_orchestrator``; see\n49\t``aria-kernel/tests/invariants/v5/_helpers.py`` for the canonical\n50\tmock fakes covering all V5.2 verdict paths.\n51\t\"\"\"\n52\t\n53\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n54\t\n55\timport hashlib\n56\timport re\n57\timport time\n58\tfrom pathlib import Path\n59\tfrom typing import Any, Literal, Protocol, TypedDict\n60\t\n61\tfrom .agent_invocations import (\n62\t    accepted_result_for_request,\n63\t    create_agent_invocation_request,\n64\t    derive_request_state,\n65\t)\n66\tfrom .agent_surface import TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES\n67\tfrom .tool_registry import ensure_tools_dir\n68\t\n69\t# The judge's sealed response must carry an explicit machine-readable\n70\t# verdict line. Transport acceptance of a submission says the judge RAN —\n71\t# never what it concluded; before this contract existed, any accepted\n72\t# row manufactured a `no_gaps` and auto-merge read a review that never\n73\t# happened (audit ARIA-AUDIT-079). Fail-closed by construction: no line,\n74\t# unreadable payload, or hash drift all grade gaps_open.\n75\t_JUDGE_VERDICT_RE = re.compile(\n76\t    r\"^\\s*VERDICT:\\s*(no_gaps|gaps_open)\\s*$\",\n77\t    re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE,\n78\t)\n79\t\n80\t\n81\tdef _judge_verdict_from_payload(payload: str) -> str | None:\n82\t    match = _JUDGE_VERDICT_RE.search(payload)\n83\t    return match.group(1).lower() if match else None\n84\t\n85\t\n86\t# Plan ARIA-V5 §3d v2 — judge agent identifiers (mint envelopes for\n87\t# these target agents at the corresponding roles).\n88\t_ADVERSARIAL_JUDGE_AGENT = \"aria-adversarial-judge\"\n89\t_EVIDENCE_JUDGE_AGENT = \"aria-evidence-judge\"\n90\t_ADVERSARIAL_ROLE = \"adversarial_judgment\"\n91\t_EVIDENCE_ROLE = \"evidence_judgment\"\n92\t\n93\t# ORPHAN-HIGH-422 — terminal derived states that will never produce an\n94\t# accepted result. Reaching one of these ends the wait immediately: no\n95\t# amount of further polling turns a rejection or an escalation into a\n96\t# review. ``ACCEPTED`` is deliberately absent — that path is detected by\n97\t# the presence of the accepted result row itself, not by the state string,\n98\t# so a state that says ACCEPTED without a readable result row still fails\n99\t# closed.\n100\t# ORPHAN-HIGH-496 — DERIVED from the terminal-state SSoT, not a copy of it.\n101\t# This was a hand-maintained list and it silently fell behind when\n102\t# ANCHOR_STALE was added: an anchor-refused request can never produce an\n103\t# accepted result, but the poll loop did not recognise the state and burned\n104\t# its full judge_timeout_seconds waiting for one. Every terminal state except\n105\t# ACCEPTED is non-delivering by definition, so the exception is what gets\n106\t# named and a future terminal state joins automatically.\n107\t_NON_DELIVERING_TERMINAL_STATES: frozenset[str] = TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES - {\"ACCEPTED\"}\n108\t\n109\t\n110\tclass ReviewResult(TypedDict):\n111\t    \"\"\"Plan ARIA-V5 §3d v2 — review runner return contract.\"\"\"\n112\t\n113\t    plan_id: str\n114\t    impl_artifacts_ref: str\n115\t    review_verdict: Literal[\n116\t        \"no_gaps\",\n117\t        \"gaps_open\",\n118\t        \"max_review_rounds\",\n119\t        \"judge_split\",\n120\t        \"artifact_mismatch\",\n121\t    ]\n122\t    rounds_count: int\n123\t    gaps_found: list[dict[str, Any]]\n124\t    request_ids: list[str]\n125\t    convergence_id: str\n126\t    # ORPHAN-HIGH-422 — the accepted result a ``no_gaps`` verdict was\n127\t    # derived from (request_id, role, agent_id, output_hash,\n128\t    # transcript_hash). ``None`` on every non-``no_gaps`` verdict, so a\n129\t    # consumer can tell an evidenced pass from an inferred one.\n130\t    accepted_result_ref: dict[str, Any] | None\n131\t\n132\t\n133\tclass ReviewRunner(Protocol):\n134\t    \"\"\"Plan ARIA-V5 §3d v2 — injection-seam contract for the post-\n135\t    implementation review loop.\n136\t\n137\t    Why a Protocol vs a Callable type alias: same rationale as\n138\t    ``ConvergenceRunner`` — Protocol forces every mock + production\n139\t    runner to expose the EXACT keyword-only kwargs by name, making\n140\t    the contract structural."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"1c906bfa-d9fa-4731-81b3-165ad1ae7301","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:36:21.020Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/review_runner.py","content":"\"\"\"Plan ARIA-V5 §2 V5.2 — Gate B post-implementation adversarial review runner.\n\nV5.2 wires the post-implementation adversarial review loop into the\nautonomy orchestrator. After ``worker_drainer`` completes (workers\nhave claimed assignments, verified, and produced implementation\nartifacts), the orchestrator calls ``review_runner`` BEFORE\n``auto_merge_runner`` — gating auto-merge on\n``review_verdict == \"no_gaps\"``.\n\nOperator vision (Plan ARIA-V5 §1, verbatim):\n  \"ımplementerler ımplement ettıkten sonra da eksık varmı yanlıs varmı\n   dıye agentlar yıne kontrol etmelı\"\n\nThe drainer mints adversarial_judge + evidence_judge envelopes\n(post-impl judgment roles already in ``agent_invocations.ROLES``).\nDefault loop:\n\n  1. round 1 — mint adversarial_judgment envelope; poll for\n     submission until ``judge_timeout_seconds`` expires\n  2. if adversarial judge returns gaps → mint evidence_judgment\n     envelope (second opinion); combine verdicts\n  3. if both judges say no_gaps → return ``no_gaps``\n  4. if judges disagree → return ``judge_split``\n  5. loop to next round if gaps_open up to ``max_review_rounds``\n  6. on cap → return ``max_review_rounds``\n  7. if cycle_id ↔ impl_artifacts_ref hash mismatch → return\n     ``artifact_mismatch``\n\nDefensive default: when no real adversarial / evidence judge is\nclaiming envelopes (typical autonomous-run mode without real Claude Code\n/ Claude Code dispatchers), the drainer times out at ``gaps_open``;\nthe orchestrator then skips ``auto_merge_runner`` cleanly. This is\nthe correct fail-closed behaviour — no review consensus, no\nauto-merge. Future V6+ work wires real judges to consume the\nenvelopes.\n\nORPHAN-HIGH-422 evidence invariant:\n  ``no_gaps`` is derived ONLY from an accepted result row bound to the\n  round's own request id AND role, carried back on\n  ``ReviewResult.accepted_result_ref``. Pre-fix the loop inferred success\n  from ``next_pending_request(...) is None`` — but a request leaves the\n  pending set on claim, rejection, cancellation, staleness and\n  HUMAN_REQUIRED escalation, so the one signal meaning \"a human must look\n  at this\" was the signal that cleared the gate. Absence of work waiting\n  is not evidence that work was done.\n\nTests inject mock review runners via the ``review_runner`` kwarg on\n``run_autonomy_orchestrator``; see\n``aria-kernel/tests/invariants/v5/_helpers.py`` for the canonical\nmock fakes covering all V5.2 verdict paths.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Literal, Protocol, TypedDict\n\nfrom .agent_invocations import (\n    accepted_result_for_request,\n    create_agent_invocation_request,\n    derive_request_state,\n)\nfrom .agent_surface import TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES\nfrom .tool_registry import ensure_tools_dir\n\n# The judge's sealed response must carry an explicit machine-readable\n# verdict line. Transport acceptance of a submission says the judge RAN —\n# never what it concluded; before this contract existed, any accepted\n# row manufactured a `no_gaps` and auto-merge read a review that never\n# happened (audit ARIA-AUDIT-079). Fail-closed by construction: no line,\n# unreadable payload, or hash drift all grade gaps_open.\n_JUDGE_VERDICT_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"^\\s*VERDICT:\\s*(no_gaps|gaps_open)\\s*$\",\n    re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE,\n)\n\n\ndef _judge_verdict_from_payload(payload: str) -> str | None:\n    match = _JUDGE_VERDICT_RE.search(payload)\n    return match.group(1).lower() if match else None\n\n\n# Plan ARIA-V5 §3d v2 — judge agent identifiers (mint envelopes for\n# these target agents at the corresponding roles).\n_ADVERSARIAL_JUDGE_AGENT = \"aria-adversarial-judge\"\n_EVIDENCE_JUDGE_AGENT = \"aria-evidence-judge\"\n_ADVERSARIAL_ROLE = \"adversarial_judgment\"\n_EVIDENCE_ROLE = \"evidence_judgment\"\n\n# ORPHAN-HIGH-422 — terminal derived states that will never produce an\n# accepted result. Reaching one of these ends the wait immediately: no\n# amount of further polling turns a rejection or an escalation into a\n# review. ``ACCEPTED`` is deliberately absent — that path is detected by\n# the presence of the accepted result row itself, not by the state string,\n# so a state that says ACCEPTED without a readable result row still fails\n# closed.\n# ORPHAN-HIGH-496 — DERIVED from the terminal-state SSoT, not a copy of it.\n# This was a hand-maintained list and it silently fell behind when\n# ANCHOR_STALE was added: an anchor-refused request can never produce an\n# accepted result, but the poll loop did not recognise the state and burned\n# its full judge_timeout_seconds waiting for one. Every terminal state except\n# ACCEPTED is non-delivering by definition, so the exception is what gets\n# named and a future terminal state joins automatically.\n_NON_DELIVERING_TERMINAL_STATES: frozenset[str] = TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES - {\"ACCEPTED\"}\n\n\nclass ReviewResult(TypedDict):\n    \"\"\"Plan ARIA-V5 §3d v2 — review runner return contract.\"\"\"\n\n    plan_id: str\n    impl_artifacts_ref: str\n    review_verdict: Literal[\n        \"no_gaps\",\n        \"gaps_open\",\n        \"max_review_rounds\",\n        \"judge_split\",\n        \"artifact_mismatch\",\n    ]\n    rounds_count: int\n    gaps_found: list[dict[str, Any]]\n    request_ids: list[str]\n    convergence_id: str\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-422 — the accepted result a ``no_gaps`` verdict was\n    # derived from (request_id, role, agent_id, output_hash,\n    # transcript_hash). ``None`` on every non-``no_gaps`` verdict, so a\n    # consumer can tell an evidenced pass from an inferred one.\n    accepted_result_ref: dict[str, Any] | None\n\n\nclass ReviewRunner(Protocol):\n    \"\"\"Plan ARIA-V5 §3d v2 — injection-seam contract for the post-\n    implementation review loop.\n\n    Why a Protocol vs a Callable type alias: same rationale as\n    ``ConvergenceRunner`` — Protocol forces every mock + production\n    runner to expose the EXACT keyword-only kwargs by name, making\n    the contract structural.","numLines":140,"startLine":1,"totalLines":481}}}
