{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JR6xH8Ypebyh5eopR3swDF","type":"tool_result","content":"1315\t\n1316\t\n1317\t# Plan 026R §E.10 — forbidden direct-to-ACTIVE source states.\n1318\t# Lifecycle invariant: a tool can only reach ACTIVE via the documented\n1319\t# SHADOW -> ACTIVE promotion path (with precision threshold + operator\n1320\t# approval). Pre-§E.10 the kernel only checked CALIBRATE explicitly;\n1321\t# every other source state silently succeeded.\n1322\t_FORBIDDEN_ACTIVE_SOURCES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n1323\t    \"DRAFT\", \"SANDBOX\", \"ARCHIVED\", \"QUARANTINED\", \"CALIBRATE\",\n1324\t})\n1325\t\n1326\t\n1327\tdef transition_tool(\n1328\t    tool_id: str,\n1329\t    target_status: str,\n1330\t    *,\n1331\t    reason: str,\n1332\t    base_dir: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = None,\n1333\t    root_cause_note: str | None = None,\n1334\t    fixture_update_ref: str | None = None,\n1335\t    fixture_suite_passed: bool = False,\n1336\t    operator_approval: bool = False,\n1337\t    auto_promote_token: str | None = None,\n1338\t    panel_approval_token: str | None = None,\n1339\t    precision: float | None = None,\n1340\t    critical_false_positives: int = 0,\n1341\t    evidence_chains_valid: bool = False,\n1342\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n1343\t    \"\"\"Plan ARIA-V6 §2e v2 B-V1-1 — auto_promote_token added.\n1344\t\n1345\t    The ``auto_promote_token`` kwarg substitutes for ``operator_\n1346\t    approval`` ONLY when the genesis_policy ``auto_promote`` block is\n1347\t    enabled, the runtime profile is ``autonomous``, the adapter's\n1348\t    precision history shows ≥ ``min_precision`` over ≥\n1349\t    ``min_clean_cycles`` consecutive runs, and ``critical_false_\n1350\t    positives`` over that window is zero. The token is generated by\n1351\t    ``adapter_calibration.compute_auto_promote_token(tool_id, base_dir)``\n1352\t    which inspects the precision_history ledger; tamper-evident hash\n1353\t    over (tool_id, last_N runs, base_dir contract hash).\n1354\t\n1355\t    JJ-2b (ORPHAN-HIGH-732) — ``panel_approval_token`` is the THIRD\n1356\t    authority. Operator directive 2026-08-18: promotion to ACTIVE is\n1357\t    PANEL-APPROVED with a 24-hour operator VETO window, not operator-\n1358\t    approved. The token is minted by ``promotion_veto.compute_panel_\n1359\t    approval_token`` ONLY after the kernel has re-derived the panel approval\n1360\t    from the human-required adjudication record (exists, resolved, resolved_\n1361\t    by=agent_panel, kind=tool_promotion, context.tool_id matches) AND the\n1362\t    veto window elapsed with no veto recorded. A kernel-scoped adapter can\n1363\t    never obtain one — that exception is enforced at mint time, where the\n1364\t    scope is readable, and kernel scope is decided by the runtime glob\n1365\t    evaluator, not by how the manifest spells its globs.\n1366\t\n1367\t    What the token IS, since the forged-token reproduction: a value this\n1368\t    function VERIFIES, exactly like the auto-promote token since\n1369\t    ORPHAN-HIGH-787. It is a workspace-bound HMAC whose verification\n1370\t    originally lived only at its mint — ``promotion_veto.compute_panel_\n1371\t    approval_token`` re-derives the panel approval from the human-required\n1372\t    adjudication record before signing — but ``transition_tool`` sees\n1373\t    caller input, and a truthy-but-forged string passed the literal\n1374\t    predicate as authority. ``promotion_veto.verify_panel_approval_token``\n1375\t    now re-derives the mint and compares MACs at consume time, so a forged\n1376\t    string reads as \"no token\" and the gates stay upstream (mint) AND\n1377\t    downstream (consume).\n1378\t\n1379\t    The literal predicate (I-V6.4-04 source-substring invariant pins):\n1380\t\n1381\t        if (not operator_approval and not _auto_promote_verified and not _panel_promote_verified) or not evidence_chains_valid:\n1382\t\n1383\t    preserves V5's evidence_chains_valid check unchanged; no authority\n1384\t    can bypass evidence chain integrity — it is still the LAST clause and\n1385\t    still short-circuits independently of who vouched. Precision + FP\n1386\t    thresholds above this line are also UNCHANGED.\n1387\t    \"\"\"\n1388\t    if target_status not in TOOL_STATUSES:\n1389\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"unknown lifecycle state: {target_status}\")\n1390\t    if not reason:\n1391\t        raise GovernanceError(\"transition reason is required\")\n1392\t\n1393\t    tool = get_tool(tool_id, base_dir)\n1394\t    if target_status in RUNNER_REQUIRED_STATUSES and \"runner\" not in tool:\n1395\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"{target_status} tool requires runner configuration\")\n1396\t    current = tool[\"status\"]\n1397\t    if current == \"QUARANTINED\" and target_status == \"CALIBRATE\":\n1398\t        if not root_cause_note or not fixture_update_ref:\n1399\t            raise GovernanceError(\n1400\t                \"QUARANTINED -> CALIBRATE requires root_cause_note and fixture_update_ref\",\n1401\t            )\n1402\t    if current == \"CALIBRATE\" and target_status == \"SHADOW\" and not fixture_suite_passed:\n1403\t        raise GovernanceError(\"CALIBRATE -> SHADOW requires fixture_suite_passed\")\n1404\t    if target_status == \"ACTIVE\":\n1405\t        # Plan 026R §E.10 — explicit lifecycle matrix. Pre-§E.10 only\n1406\t        # ``CALIBRATE → ACTIVE`` and ``SHADOW → ACTIVE`` were\n1407\t        # explicitly checked; ``DRAFT → ACTIVE`` /\n1408\t        # ``SANDBOX → ACTIVE`` / ``ARCHIVED → ACTIVE`` /\n1409\t        # ``QUARANTINED → ACTIVE`` silently succeeded because no\n1410\t        # branch handled them. The matrix below names every forbidden\n1411\t        # source explicitly so the kernel rejects the typo /\n1412\t        # malicious / accident.\n1413\t        if current in _FORBIDDEN_ACTIVE_SOURCES:\n1414\t            raise GovernanceError(\n1415\t                f\"tool_lifecycle_forbidden_active_promotion: \"\n1416\t                f\"{current} -> ACTIVE is not permitted. \"\n1417\t                f\"Forbidden sources: {sorted(_FORBIDDEN_ACTIVE_SOURCES)}. \"\n1418\t                f\"Use the documented lifecycle path (e.g. SHADOW -> \"\n1419\t                f\"ACTIVE with precision threshold + operator approval).\"\n1420\t            )\n1421\t        threshold = float(tool[\"health_thresholds\"].get(\"precision_min\", 0.85))\n1422\t        if current == \"SHADOW\":\n1423\t            if precision is None or precision < threshold:\n1424\t                raise GovernanceError(\"SHADOW -> ACTIVE requires precision above threshold\")\n1425\t            if critical_false_positives > 0:\n1426\t                raise GovernanceError(\"SHADOW -> ACTIVE requires zero critical false positives\")\n1427\t            # Plan ARIA-V6 §2e v2 B-V1-1 — literal predicate pinned by\n1428\t            # I-V6.4-04 source-substring invariant. The order of the\n1429\t            # boolean clauses is load-bearing: evidence_chains_valid\n1430\t            # is checked LAST so it short-circuits independently of\n1431\t            # the operator_approval / auto_promote_token /\n1432\t            # panel_approval_token path. A refactor that reorders OR\n1433\t            # drops any clause silently weakens the SHADOW -> ACTIVE\n1434\t            # gate. JJ-2b added the panel clause; the pin was rewritten\n1435\t            # with it, never deleted to make a test pass.\n1436\t            # ORPHAN-HIGH-787 — the auto-promote token is VERIFIED, not\n1437\t            # counted: verify_auto_promote_token recomputes the\n1438\t            # workspace-bound HMAC over the envelope's payload and its\n1439\t            # tool binding, so a fabricated string, a cross-workspace\n1440\t            # replay or a cross-tool replay all read as \"no token\".\n1441\t            # Late import: adapter_calibration reads tool_registry at\n1442\t            # module level, so a top-level import would be a cycle.\n1443\t            _auto_promote_verified = False\n1444\t            if auto_promote_token:\n1445\t                from .adapter_calibration import verify_auto_promote_token\n1446\t\n1447\t                _auto_promote_verified = (\n1448\t                    verify_auto_promote_token(\n1449\t                        auto_promote_token, tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=base_dir\n1450\t                    )\n1451\t                    is not None\n1452\t                )\n1453\t            # Same standard for the panel token, symmetric with the auto\n1454\t            # lane: presence is not authority. verify_panel_approval_token\n1455\t            # re-derives the mint (kernel scope, panel adjudication, veto\n1456\t            # window) and compares MACs in constant time, so a forged\n1457\t            # string reads as \"no token\" instead of as a promotion.\n1458\t            # Late import for the same cycle reason as above.\n1459\t            _panel_promote_verified = False\n1460\t            if panel_approval_token:\n1461\t                from .promotion_veto import verify_panel_approval_token\n1462\t\n1463\t                _panel_promote_verified = (\n1464\t                    verify_panel_approval_token(\n1465\t                        panel_approval_token, tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=base_dir\n1466\t                    )\n1467\t                    is not None\n1468\t                )\n1469\t            if (not operator_approval and not _auto_promote_verified and not _panel_promote_verified) or not evidence_chains_valid:\n1470\t                raise GovernanceError(\n1471\t                    \"SHADOW -> ACTIVE requires valid evidence chains and \"\n1472\t                    \"(operator_approval OR auto_promote_token OR \"\n1473\t                    \"panel_approval_token under safe conditions)\",\n1474\t                )\n1475\t\n1476\t    # Plan 022 §C-2b — transition_tool is the audited state machine;\n1477\t    # write its own status + last_transition update via the internal\n1478\t    # primitive so the public update_tool() guard does not self-block.\n1479\t    return _update_tool_internal(\n1480\t        tool_id,\n1481\t        {\n1482\t            \"status\": target_status,\n1483\t            \"last_transition\": {\n1484\t                \"at\": utc_now(),"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"00c33c56-5eea-45af-b6e1-bd4e0a96bd07","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:36:22.208Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/tool_registry.py","content":"\n\n# Plan 026R §E.10 — forbidden direct-to-ACTIVE source states.\n# Lifecycle invariant: a tool can only reach ACTIVE via the documented\n# SHADOW -> ACTIVE promotion path (with precision threshold + operator\n# approval). Pre-§E.10 the kernel only checked CALIBRATE explicitly;\n# every other source state silently succeeded.\n_FORBIDDEN_ACTIVE_SOURCES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n    \"DRAFT\", \"SANDBOX\", \"ARCHIVED\", \"QUARANTINED\", \"CALIBRATE\",\n})\n\n\ndef transition_tool(\n    tool_id: str,\n    target_status: str,\n    *,\n    reason: str,\n    base_dir: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = None,\n    root_cause_note: str | None = None,\n    fixture_update_ref: str | None = None,\n    fixture_suite_passed: bool = False,\n    operator_approval: bool = False,\n    auto_promote_token: str | None = None,\n    panel_approval_token: str | None = None,\n    precision: float | None = None,\n    critical_false_positives: int = 0,\n    evidence_chains_valid: bool = False,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Plan ARIA-V6 §2e v2 B-V1-1 — auto_promote_token added.\n\n    The ``auto_promote_token`` kwarg substitutes for ``operator_\n    approval`` ONLY when the genesis_policy ``auto_promote`` block is\n    enabled, the runtime profile is ``autonomous``, the adapter's\n    precision history shows ≥ ``min_precision`` over ≥\n    ``min_clean_cycles`` consecutive runs, and ``critical_false_\n    positives`` over that window is zero. The token is generated by\n    ``adapter_calibration.compute_auto_promote_token(tool_id, base_dir)``\n    which inspects the precision_history ledger; tamper-evident hash\n    over (tool_id, last_N runs, base_dir contract hash).\n\n    JJ-2b (ORPHAN-HIGH-732) — ``panel_approval_token`` is the THIRD\n    authority. Operator directive 2026-08-18: promotion to ACTIVE is\n    PANEL-APPROVED with a 24-hour operator VETO window, not operator-\n    approved. The token is minted by ``promotion_veto.compute_panel_\n    approval_token`` ONLY after the kernel has re-derived the panel approval\n    from the human-required adjudication record (exists, resolved, resolved_\n    by=agent_panel, kind=tool_promotion, context.tool_id matches) AND the\n    veto window elapsed with no veto recorded. A kernel-scoped adapter can\n    never obtain one — that exception is enforced at mint time, where the\n    scope is readable, and kernel scope is decided by the runtime glob\n    evaluator, not by how the manifest spells its globs.\n\n    What the token IS, since the forged-token reproduction: a value this\n    function VERIFIES, exactly like the auto-promote token since\n    ORPHAN-HIGH-787. It is a workspace-bound HMAC whose verification\n    originally lived only at its mint — ``promotion_veto.compute_panel_\n    approval_token`` re-derives the panel approval from the human-required\n    adjudication record before signing — but ``transition_tool`` sees\n    caller input, and a truthy-but-forged string passed the literal\n    predicate as authority. ``promotion_veto.verify_panel_approval_token``\n    now re-derives the mint and compares MACs at consume time, so a forged\n    string reads as \"no token\" and the gates stay upstream (mint) AND\n    downstream (consume).\n\n    The literal predicate (I-V6.4-04 source-substring invariant pins):\n\n        if (not operator_approval and not _auto_promote_verified and not _panel_promote_verified) or not evidence_chains_valid:\n\n    preserves V5's evidence_chains_valid check unchanged; no authority\n    can bypass evidence chain integrity — it is still the LAST clause and\n    still short-circuits independently of who vouched. Precision + FP\n    thresholds above this line are also UNCHANGED.\n    \"\"\"\n    if target_status not in TOOL_STATUSES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"unknown lifecycle state: {target_status}\")\n    if not reason:\n        raise GovernanceError(\"transition reason is required\")\n\n    tool = get_tool(tool_id, base_dir)\n    if target_status in RUNNER_REQUIRED_STATUSES and \"runner\" not in tool:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"{target_status} tool requires runner configuration\")\n    current = tool[\"status\"]\n    if current == \"QUARANTINED\" and target_status == \"CALIBRATE\":\n        if not root_cause_note or not fixture_update_ref:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                \"QUARANTINED -> CALIBRATE requires root_cause_note and fixture_update_ref\",\n            )\n    if current == \"CALIBRATE\" and target_status == \"SHADOW\" and not fixture_suite_passed:\n        raise GovernanceError(\"CALIBRATE -> SHADOW requires fixture_suite_passed\")\n    if target_status == \"ACTIVE\":\n        # Plan 026R §E.10 — explicit lifecycle matrix. Pre-§E.10 only\n        # ``CALIBRATE → ACTIVE`` and ``SHADOW → ACTIVE`` were\n        # explicitly checked; ``DRAFT → ACTIVE`` /\n        # ``SANDBOX → ACTIVE`` / ``ARCHIVED → ACTIVE`` /\n        # ``QUARANTINED → ACTIVE`` silently succeeded because no\n        # branch handled them. The matrix below names every forbidden\n        # source explicitly so the kernel rejects the typo /\n        # malicious / accident.\n        if current in _FORBIDDEN_ACTIVE_SOURCES:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"tool_lifecycle_forbidden_active_promotion: \"\n                f\"{current} -> ACTIVE is not permitted. \"\n                f\"Forbidden sources: {sorted(_FORBIDDEN_ACTIVE_SOURCES)}. \"\n                f\"Use the documented lifecycle path (e.g. SHADOW -> \"\n                f\"ACTIVE with precision threshold + operator approval).\"\n            )\n        threshold = float(tool[\"health_thresholds\"].get(\"precision_min\", 0.85))\n        if current == \"SHADOW\":\n            if precision is None or precision < threshold:\n                raise GovernanceError(\"SHADOW -> ACTIVE requires precision above threshold\")\n            if critical_false_positives > 0:\n                raise GovernanceError(\"SHADOW -> ACTIVE requires zero critical false positives\")\n            # Plan ARIA-V6 §2e v2 B-V1-1 — literal predicate pinned by\n            # I-V6.4-04 source-substring invariant. The order of the\n            # boolean clauses is load-bearing: evidence_chains_valid\n            # is checked LAST so it short-circuits independently of\n            # the operator_approval / auto_promote_token /\n            # panel_approval_token path. A refactor that reorders OR\n            # drops any clause silently weakens the SHADOW -> ACTIVE\n            # gate. JJ-2b added the panel clause; the pin was rewritten\n            # with it, never deleted to make a test pass.\n            # ORPHAN-HIGH-787 — the auto-promote token is VERIFIED, not\n            # counted: verify_auto_promote_token recomputes the\n            # workspace-bound HMAC over the envelope's payload and its\n            # tool binding, so a fabricated string, a cross-workspace\n            # replay or a cross-tool replay all read as \"no token\".\n            # Late import: adapter_calibration reads tool_registry at\n            # module level, so a top-level import would be a cycle.\n            _auto_promote_verified = False\n            if auto_promote_token:\n                from .adapter_calibration import verify_auto_promote_token\n\n                _auto_promote_verified = (\n                    verify_auto_promote_token(\n                        auto_promote_token, tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=base_dir\n                    )\n                    is not None\n                )\n            # Same standard for the panel token, symmetric with the auto\n            # lane: presence is not authority. verify_panel_approval_token\n            # re-derives the mint (kernel scope, panel adjudication, veto\n            # window) and compares MACs in constant time, so a forged\n            # string reads as \"no token\" instead of as a promotion.\n            # Late import for the same cycle reason as above.\n            _panel_promote_verified = False\n            if panel_approval_token:\n                from .promotion_veto import verify_panel_approval_token\n\n                _panel_promote_verified = (\n                    verify_panel_approval_token(\n                        panel_approval_token, tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=base_dir\n                    )\n                    is not None\n                )\n            if (not operator_approval and not _auto_promote_verified and not _panel_promote_verified) or not evidence_chains_valid:\n                raise GovernanceError(\n                    \"SHADOW -> ACTIVE requires valid evidence chains and \"\n                    \"(operator_approval OR auto_promote_token OR \"\n                    \"panel_approval_token under safe conditions)\",\n                )\n\n    # Plan 022 §C-2b — transition_tool is the audited state machine;\n    # write its own status + last_transition update via the internal\n    # primitive so the public update_tool() guard does not self-block.\n    return _update_tool_internal(\n        tool_id,\n        {\n            \"status\": target_status,\n            \"last_transition\": {\n                \"at\": utc_now(),","numLines":170,"startLine":1315,"totalLines":1518}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RbcgT7bjfajz2nx2Ev45Nd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Plan 025 §A — guarantee >=2 distinct judges per sampled finding.\n2\t\n3\t``generate_judgment_sample`` produces a human worklist, not judge dispatch: it\n4\tmints no ``judge_id``, no ``judgment_group_id``, no envelopes. So for tool\n5\tfindings the kernel never fanned out to two judges — a finding could get 0/1\n6\t``ai_judge`` row and sit as ``single_judge`` forever\n7\t(``feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus`` requires >=2 unique judges), starving\n8\tconsensus and calibration of data.\n9\t\n10\tThis module turns each sampled finding into TWO judge invocation envelopes\n11\t(``aria-evidence-judge`` + ``aria-adversarial-judge``) sharing one\n12\t``judgment_group_id`` with distinct ``judge_id``, so consensus can fire by\n13\tconstruction once the judges respond. Idempotent: ``create_agent_invocation_request``\n14\treturns the existing request for identical args, and a finding whose group is\n15\talready dispatched is skipped.\n16\t\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom pathlib import Path\n20\tfrom typing import Any\n21\t\n22\tfrom .agent_invocations import create_agent_invocation_request\n23\tfrom .ledger import load_declared_jsonl\n24\tfrom .tool_registry import ensure_tools_dir\n25\t\n26\t\n27\t# Two distinct judge roles → two distinct judge_ids on the same finding. The\n28\t# kernel's consensus gate needs exactly this to ever fire on a tool finding.\n29\tJUDGE_FANOUT: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (\n30\t    (\"evidence_judgment\", \"aria-evidence-judge\"),\n31\t    (\"adversarial_judgment\", \"aria-adversarial-judge\"),\n32\t)\n33\t\n34\t# E14 — the arbiter that the `consensus_arbitration` role always named and\n35\t# nothing ever minted. The fan-out above guarantees two judges; when those two\n36\t# disagree the mechanical gate (`feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus`) records\n37\t# a `judge_disagreement` uncertainty and the finding's verdict stops there. The\n38\t# arbiter is the tie-break: it reads the two verdicts, applies the same\n39\t# consensus gate the kernel applies, and its answer lands in the SAME judgment\n40\t# group as a third calibrated vote, so a weighted gate can settle the finding.\n41\tCONSENSUS_ARBITRATION_ROLE: str = \"consensus_arbitration\"\n42\tCONSENSUS_ARBITER_AGENT: str = \"aria-consensus-arbiter\"\n43\t\n44\t# JJ-1 (ORPHAN-HIGH-731) - the SECOND reason to mint the arbiter: not a\n45\t# split, an ANCHOR. The split arm above only ever fires when the two judges\n46\t# disagree, so a consensus that will become ground truth was, until now,\n47\t# exactly the case nobody examined - agreement was treated as proof instead\n48\t# of as the thing to test. The anchor mint asks the arbiter to REFUTE a\n49\t# unanimous pair; surviving that is what promotes the row to ground truth\n50\t# (feedback_store.is_ground_truth_row). Same role, same agent, same\n51\t# (judgment_group_id, target_agent) idempotency key as the split arm, so a\n52\t# group can never carry two arbiters for two reasons.\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef _finding_key(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n56\t    \"\"\"The run-independent identity of the finding being judged.\"\"\"\n57\t    return str(item.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or item.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef _group_id(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n61\t    \"\"\"Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — the judgment group is keyed by the FINDING, not the run.\n62\t\n63\t    The shipped key folded ``run_id`` in, so every nightly adapter run\n64\t    re-minted fresh envelopes for findings already sitting in the queue —\n65\t    462 minted, 42 accepted, 296 dead of anchor staleness in one week. With\n66\t    the run out of the key, the (group, agent) dedupe below becomes\n67\t    cross-night: a finding is asked once and re-examined only by the E9\n68\t    decision-questioning lane, which exists for exactly that.\n69\t    \"\"\"\n70\t    return f\"judge:{item.get('tool_id')}:{_finding_key(item)}\"\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef _render_prompt(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n74\t    return (\n75\t        \"Judge whether this adapter finding is a true_positive or false_positive.\\n\"\n76\t        f\"finding_id: {item.get('finding_id')}\\n\"\n77\t        f\"rule: {item.get('rule')}\\n\"\n78\t        f\"severity: {item.get('severity')}\\n\"\n79\t        f\"path: {item.get('path')}\\n\"\n80\t        f\"message: {item.get('message')}\\n\"\n81\t        \"Return verdict true_positive|false_positive with file:line evidence.\"\n82\t    )\n83\t\n84\t\n85\tdef _evidence_refs(item: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:\n86\t    refs: list[str] = []\n87\t    path = str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\")\n88\t    if path:\n89\t        refs.append(path)\n90\t    for ev in item.get(\"evidence\") or []:\n91\t        if isinstance(ev, str) and ev.strip():\n92\t            refs.append(ev)\n93\t    return refs\n94\t\n95\t\n96\tdef _existing_judge_dispatches(root: Path) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:\n97\t    \"\"\"Already-minted (judgment_group_id, target_agent) pairs. Keyed per-agent\n98\t    (not per-group) so a group left with only one judge — e.g. the second mint\n99\t    raised mid-loop last cycle — gets its missing judge minted next run instead\n100\t    of being skipped forever and starving consensus.\"\"\"\n101\t    try:\n102\t        rows = load_declared_jsonl(\n103\t            root / \"agent-invocations\" / \"requests.jsonl\",\n104\t            expected_surface=\"agent_invocation_requests\",\n105\t        )\n106\t    except Exception:\n107\t        return set()\n108\t    return {\n109\t        (str(r.get(\"judgment_group_id\")), str(r.get(\"target_agent\")))\n110\t        for r in rows\n111\t        if r.get(\"judgment_group_id\") and r.get(\"target_agent\")\n112\t    }\n113\t\n114\t\n115\tdef _judged_pairs(root: Path, tool_id: str) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:\n116\t    \"\"\"(finding_key, judge_id) pairs that already carry an ai_judge verdict.\n117\t\n118\t    A finding a judge has ALREADY answered must not be re-minted by the\n119\t    nightly fan-out regardless of ledger key drift — re-sampling closed\n120\t    verdicts is the decision-questioning lane's deliberate act, not the"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"18c939e9-29a0-4400-90e7-d6ac9edf0281","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:36:34.101Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judge_fanout.py","content":"\"\"\"Plan 025 §A — guarantee >=2 distinct judges per sampled finding.\n\n``generate_judgment_sample`` produces a human worklist, not judge dispatch: it\nmints no ``judge_id``, no ``judgment_group_id``, no envelopes. So for tool\nfindings the kernel never fanned out to two judges — a finding could get 0/1\n``ai_judge`` row and sit as ``single_judge`` forever\n(``feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus`` requires >=2 unique judges), starving\nconsensus and calibration of data.\n\nThis module turns each sampled finding into TWO judge invocation envelopes\n(``aria-evidence-judge`` + ``aria-adversarial-judge``) sharing one\n``judgment_group_id`` with distinct ``judge_id``, so consensus can fire by\nconstruction once the judges respond. Idempotent: ``create_agent_invocation_request``\nreturns the existing request for identical args, and a finding whose group is\nalready dispatched is skipped.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom .agent_invocations import create_agent_invocation_request\nfrom .ledger import load_declared_jsonl\nfrom .tool_registry import ensure_tools_dir\n\n\n# Two distinct judge roles → two distinct judge_ids on the same finding. The\n# kernel's consensus gate needs exactly this to ever fire on a tool finding.\nJUDGE_FANOUT: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (\n    (\"evidence_judgment\", \"aria-evidence-judge\"),\n    (\"adversarial_judgment\", \"aria-adversarial-judge\"),\n)\n\n# E14 — the arbiter that the `consensus_arbitration` role always named and\n# nothing ever minted. The fan-out above guarantees two judges; when those two\n# disagree the mechanical gate (`feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus`) records\n# a `judge_disagreement` uncertainty and the finding's verdict stops there. The\n# arbiter is the tie-break: it reads the two verdicts, applies the same\n# consensus gate the kernel applies, and its answer lands in the SAME judgment\n# group as a third calibrated vote, so a weighted gate can settle the finding.\nCONSENSUS_ARBITRATION_ROLE: str = \"consensus_arbitration\"\nCONSENSUS_ARBITER_AGENT: str = \"aria-consensus-arbiter\"\n\n# JJ-1 (ORPHAN-HIGH-731) - the SECOND reason to mint the arbiter: not a\n# split, an ANCHOR. The split arm above only ever fires when the two judges\n# disagree, so a consensus that will become ground truth was, until now,\n# exactly the case nobody examined - agreement was treated as proof instead\n# of as the thing to test. The anchor mint asks the arbiter to REFUTE a\n# unanimous pair; surviving that is what promotes the row to ground truth\n# (feedback_store.is_ground_truth_row). Same role, same agent, same\n# (judgment_group_id, target_agent) idempotency key as the split arm, so a\n# group can never carry two arbiters for two reasons.\n\n\ndef _finding_key(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The run-independent identity of the finding being judged.\"\"\"\n    return str(item.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or item.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n\n\ndef _group_id(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — the judgment group is keyed by the FINDING, not the run.\n\n    The shipped key folded ``run_id`` in, so every nightly adapter run\n    re-minted fresh envelopes for findings already sitting in the queue —\n    462 minted, 42 accepted, 296 dead of anchor staleness in one week. With\n    the run out of the key, the (group, agent) dedupe below becomes\n    cross-night: a finding is asked once and re-examined only by the E9\n    decision-questioning lane, which exists for exactly that.\n    \"\"\"\n    return f\"judge:{item.get('tool_id')}:{_finding_key(item)}\"\n\n\ndef _render_prompt(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    return (\n        \"Judge whether this adapter finding is a true_positive or false_positive.\\n\"\n        f\"finding_id: {item.get('finding_id')}\\n\"\n        f\"rule: {item.get('rule')}\\n\"\n        f\"severity: {item.get('severity')}\\n\"\n        f\"path: {item.get('path')}\\n\"\n        f\"message: {item.get('message')}\\n\"\n        \"Return verdict true_positive|false_positive with file:line evidence.\"\n    )\n\n\ndef _evidence_refs(item: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:\n    refs: list[str] = []\n    path = str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\")\n    if path:\n        refs.append(path)\n    for ev in item.get(\"evidence\") or []:\n        if isinstance(ev, str) and ev.strip():\n            refs.append(ev)\n    return refs\n\n\ndef _existing_judge_dispatches(root: Path) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Already-minted (judgment_group_id, target_agent) pairs. Keyed per-agent\n    (not per-group) so a group left with only one judge — e.g. the second mint\n    raised mid-loop last cycle — gets its missing judge minted next run instead\n    of being skipped forever and starving consensus.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        rows = load_declared_jsonl(\n            root / \"agent-invocations\" / \"requests.jsonl\",\n            expected_surface=\"agent_invocation_requests\",\n        )\n    except Exception:\n        return set()\n    return {\n        (str(r.get(\"judgment_group_id\")), str(r.get(\"target_agent\")))\n        for r in rows\n        if r.get(\"judgment_group_id\") and r.get(\"target_agent\")\n    }\n\n\ndef _judged_pairs(root: Path, tool_id: str) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"(finding_key, judge_id) pairs that already carry an ai_judge verdict.\n\n    A finding a judge has ALREADY answered must not be re-minted by the\n    nightly fan-out regardless of ledger key drift — re-sampling closed\n    verdicts is the decision-questioning lane's deliberate act, not the","numLines":120,"startLine":1,"totalLines":661}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01D7zUEJsN5FZLFpb9yETexA","type":"tool_result","content":"120\t    verdicts is the decision-questioning lane's deliberate act, not the\n121\t    sampler's accident. Fingerprint preferred, finding_id fallback for\n122\t    rows recorded before fingerprints were threaded.\n123\t    \"\"\"\n124\t    from .feedback_store import load_feedback\n125\t\n126\t    pairs: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()\n127\t    try:\n128\t        rows = load_feedback(tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=root)\n129\t    except Exception:\n130\t        return pairs\n131\t    for row in rows:\n132\t        if row.get(\"source_type\") != \"ai_judge\":\n133\t            continue\n134\t        key = str(row.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or row.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n135\t        judge = str(row.get(\"judge_id\") or \"\")\n136\t        if key and judge:\n137\t            pairs.add((key, judge))\n138\t    return pairs\n139\t\n140\t\n141\tdef pending_judge_counts(*, base_dir: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n142\t    \"\"\"Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — live (non-terminal) envelope count per judge role.\n143\t\n144\t    Bounded to the anchor window: anything older is ANCHOR_STALE by\n145\t    definition, so deriving its state would only re-prove it dead.\n146\t    \"\"\"\n147\t    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n148\t\n149\t    from .agent_invocations import derive_request_states, list_agent_invocation_requests\n150\t    from .agent_surface import TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES\n151\t\n152\t    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n153\t    horizon = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=4)\n154\t    counts: dict[str, int] = {role: 0 for role, _ in JUDGE_FANOUT}\n155\t    # ORPHAN-HIGH-794 — one batch derivation instead of a per-row derive\n156\t    # (each of which reloaded all three ledgers — the OOM churn class).\n157\t    states = derive_request_states(base_dir=root)\n158\t    for row in list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=root):\n159\t        role = str(row.get(\"role\") or \"\")\n160\t        if role not in counts:\n161\t            continue\n162\t        created = str(row.get(\"created_at\") or \"\")\n163\t        try:\n164\t            created_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(created.replace(\"Z\", \"+00:00\"))\n165\t        except ValueError:\n166\t            created_dt = None\n167\t        if created_dt is not None and created_dt < horizon:\n168\t            continue\n169\t        state = states.get(str(row.get(\"request_id\")), \"PENDING\")\n170\t        if state not in TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES:\n171\t            counts[role] += 1\n172\t    return counts\n173\t\n174\t\n175\tdef dispatch_judges_for_sample(\n176\t    *,\n177\t    sample: dict[str, Any],\n178\t    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n179\t    target_sha: str | None = None,\n180\t    repo_root: str | Path | None = None,\n181\t    max_pending_per_role: int | None = None,\n182\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n183\t    \"\"\"Mint two judge envelopes per finding in a judgment sample.\n184\t\n185\t    Returns a summary; the envelopes are picked up asynchronously by the\n186\t    dispatcher (claim_and_dispatch_one), so the resulting ai_judge rows — and\n187\t    thus consensus — land in a later tick, not this one.\n188\t\n189\t    ``repo_root`` is the workspace the findings were produced against. Without\n190\t    it the mint cannot read the cited files, so the E17-b evidence excerpts —\n191\t    the whole reason two judges no longer have to open the same file twice —\n192\t    never attach on the one lane that dispatches two judges per finding. The\n193\t    caller already resolves this workspace for ``target_sha``; passing it here\n194\t    is what makes the packing fire in production rather than only in tests.\n195\t    \"\"\"\n196\t    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n197\t    items = sample.get(\"items\") or []\n198\t    existing = _existing_judge_dispatches(root)\n199\t    # Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — backlog gate: when the executor's drain is behind,\n200\t    # minting more envelopes only manufactures anchor-stale corpses. The\n201\t    # ceiling is per role; freshly minted envelopes count toward it within\n202\t    # this call so one large sample cannot blow through the cap.\n203\t    pending = pending_judge_counts(base_dir=root) if max_pending_per_role is not None else {}\n204\t    judged: dict[str, set[tuple[str, str]]] = {}\n205\t    minted: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n206\t    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n207\t    for item in items:\n208\t        if not isinstance(item, dict):\n209\t            continue\n210\t        group = _group_id(item)\n211\t        prompt = _render_prompt(item)\n212\t        refs = _evidence_refs(item)\n213\t        tool_id = str(item.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\")\n214\t        if tool_id not in judged:\n215\t            judged[tool_id] = _judged_pairs(root, tool_id)\n216\t        finding_key = _finding_key(item)\n217\t        for role, agent in JUDGE_FANOUT:\n218\t            if (group, agent) in existing:\n219\t                skipped.append({\"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent, \"reason\": \"already_dispatched\"})\n220\t                continue\n221\t            if (finding_key, agent) in judged[tool_id]:\n222\t                skipped.append({\"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent, \"reason\": \"already_judged\"})\n223\t                continue\n224\t            if max_pending_per_role is not None and pending.get(role, 0) >= max_pending_per_role:\n225\t                skipped.append({\n226\t                    \"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent,\n227\t                    \"reason\": \"mint_skipped_backlog\",\n228\t                    \"pending\": pending.get(role, 0),\n229\t                    \"max_pending_per_role\": max_pending_per_role,\n230\t                })\n231\t                continue\n232\t            req = create_agent_invocation_request(\n233\t                target_agent=agent,\n234\t                role=role,\n235\t                suggested_prompt=prompt,\n236\t                must_satisfy=[{\"id\": \"verdict\", \"description\": \"Return true_positive or false_positive with file:line evidence\"}],\n237\t                allowed_scope=[\"**\"],\n238\t                evidence_refs=refs or None,\n239\t                finding_id=str(item.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n240\t                # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — thread the sampler's fingerprint onto the\n241\t                # mint so the verdict bridge can source identity from the\n242\t                # request (D1) instead of trusting the judge to echo it.\n243\t                finding_fingerprint=str(item.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or \"\"),\n244\t                tool_id=str(item.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n245\t                run_id=str(item.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n246\t                judgment_group_id=group,\n247\t                cycle_id=sample.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n248\t                target_sha=target_sha,\n249\t                base_dir=root,\n250\t                context_repo_root=repo_root,\n251\t            )\n252\t            minted.append({\n253\t Fingerprint preferred, finding_id fallback for\n    rows recorded before fingerprints were threaded.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .feedback_store import load_feedback\n\n    pairs: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()\n    try:\n        rows = load_feedback(tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=root)\n    except Exception:\n        return pairs\n    for row in rows:\n        if row.get(\"source_type\") != \"ai_judge\":\n            continue\n        key = str(row.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or row.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n        judge = str(row.get(\"judge_id\") or \"\")\n        if key and judge:\n            pairs.add((key, judge))\n    return pairs\n\n\ndef pending_judge_counts(*, base_dir: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — live (non-terminal) envelope count per judge role.\n\n    Bounded to the anchor window: anything older is ANCHOR_STALE by\n    definition, so deriving its state would only re-prove it dead.\n    \"\"\"\n    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\n    from .agent_invocations import derive_request_states, list_agent_invocation_requests\n    from .agent_surface import TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES\n\n    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n    horizon = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=4)\n    counts: dict[str, int] = {role: 0 for role, _ in JUDGE_FANOUT}\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-794 — one batch derivation instead of a per-row derive\n    # (each of which reloaded all three ledgers — the OOM churn class).\n    states = derive_request_states(base_dir=root)\n    for row in list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=root):\n        role = str(row.get(\"role\") or \"\")\n        if role not in counts:\n            continue\n        created = str(row.get(\"created_at\") or \"\")\n        try:\n            created_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(created.replace(\"Z\", \"+00:00\"))\n        except ValueError:\n            created_dt = None\n        if created_dt is not None and created_dt < horizon:\n            continue\n        state = states.get(str(row.get(\"request_id\")), \"PENDING\")\n        if state not in TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES:\n            counts[role] += 1\n    return counts\n\n\ndef dispatch_judges_for_sample(\n    *,\n    sample: dict[str, Any],\n    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n    target_sha: str | None = None,\n    repo_root: str | Path | None = None,\n    max_pending_per_role: int | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Mint two judge envelopes per finding in a judgment sample.\n\n    Returns a summary; the envelopes are picked up asynchronously by the\n    dispatcher (claim_and_dispatch_one), so the resulting ai_judge rows — and\n    thus consensus — land in a later tick, not this one.\n\n    ``repo_root`` is the workspace the findings were produced against. Without\n    it the mint cannot read the cited files, so the E17-b evidence excerpts —\n    the whole reason two judges no longer have to open the same file twice —\n    never attach on the one lane that dispatches two judges per finding. The\n    caller already resolves this workspace for ``target_sha``; passing it here\n    is what makes the packing fire in production rather than only in tests.\n    \"\"\"\n    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n    items = sample.get(\"items\") or []\n    existing = _existing_judge_dispatches(root)\n    # Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — backlog gate: when the executor's drain is behind,\n    # minting more envelopes only manufactures anchor-stale corpses. The\n    # ceiling is per role; freshly minted envelopes count toward it within\n    # this call so one large sample cannot blow through the cap.\n    pending = pending_judge_counts(base_dir=root) if max_pending_per_role is not None else {}\n    judged: dict[str, set[tuple[str, str]]] = {}\n    minted: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for item in items:\n        if not isinstance(item, dict):\n            continue\n        group = _group_id(item)\n        prompt = _render_prompt(item)\n        refs = _evidence_refs(item)\n        tool_id = str(item.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\")\n        if tool_id not in judged:\n            judged[tool_id] = _judged_pairs(root, tool_id)\n        finding_key = _finding_key(item)\n        for role, agent in JUDGE_FANOUT:\n            if (group, agent) in existing:\n                skipped.append({\"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent, \"reason\": \"already_dispatched\"})\n                continue\n            if (finding_key, agent) in judged[tool_id]:\n                skipped.append({\"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent, \"reason\": \"already_judged\"})\n                continue\n            if max_pending_per_role is not None and pending.get(role, 0) >= max_pending_per_role:\n                skipped.append({\n                    \"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent,\n                    \"reason\": \"mint_skipped_backlog\",\n                    \"pending\": pending.get(role, 0),\n                    \"max_pending_per_role\": max_pending_per_role,\n                })\n                continue\n            req = create_agent_invocation_request(\n                target_agent=agent,\n                role=role,\n                suggested_prompt=prompt,\n                must_satisfy=[{\"id\": \"verdict\", \"description\": \"Return true_positive or false_positive with file:line evidence\"}],\n                allowed_scope=[\"**\"],\n                evidence_refs=refs or None,\n                finding_id=str(item.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n                # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — thread the sampler's fingerprint onto the\n                # mint so the verdict bridge can source identity from the\n                # request (D1) instead of trusting the judge to echo it.\n                finding_fingerprint=str(item.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or \"\"),\n                tool_id=str(item.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n                run_id=str(item.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n                judgment_group_id=group,\n                cycle_id=sample.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                target_sha=target_sha,\n                base_dir=root,\n                context_repo_root=repo_root,\n            )\n            minted.append({\n                \"request_id\": req.get(\"request_id\"),\n                \"role\": role,\n                \"target_agent\": agent,\n                \"judgment_group_id\": group,\n            })\n            existing.add((group, agent))\n            if max_pending_per_role is not None:\n                pending[role] = pending.get(role, 0) + 1\n    return {\"schema_version\": 1, \"minted_count\": len(minted), \"minted\": minted, \"skipped\": skipped}\n\n\ndef _judge_rows_by_group(\n    tool_id: str,\n    base_dir: str | Path | None,\n) -> tuple[\n    dict[tuple[str, str, str], dict[str, dict[str, Any]]],\n    dict[tuple[str, str, str], int],\n]:\n    \"\"\"(votes-by-judge, settled-judge-count) per judgment group.\n\n    Mirrors the grouping in ``feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus`` (same\n    key, same ``ai_judge`` filter) rather than re-deriving a second notion of\n    \"the same finding\": a group this module calls split is exactly the group\n    that engine calls ``judge_disagreement``. The settled map carries the\n    judge_count of the consensus already recorded (0 when none), which is\n    what lets the split arm skip settled groups while the anchor arm targets\n    the ones settled BELOW the anchor floor.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .feedback_store import consensus_judge_count, load_feedback\n\n    rows = load_feedback(tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=base_dir)\n    settled: dict[tuple[str, str, str], int] = {}\n    grouped: dict[tuple[str, str, str], dict[str, dict[str, Any]]] = {}\n    for row in rows:\n        key = (\n            str(row.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n            str(row.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n            str(row.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or \"\"),\n        )\n        if row.get(\"source_type\") == \"ai_consensus\":\n            settled[key] = max(settled.get(key, 0), consensus_judge_count(row))\n            continue\n        if row.get(\"source_type\") != \"ai_judge\":\n            continue\n        judge_id = str(row.get(\"judge_id\") or \"\")\n        if not key[0] or not key[1] or not judge_id:\n            continue\n        # Last row per judge wins, exactly as the consensus engine does: a\n        # judge that re-submitted must not be counted as two voters.\n        grouped.setdefault(key, {})[judge_id] = row\n    return grouped, settled\n\n\ndef split_verdict_groups(\n    *,\n    tool_id: str,\n    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Judgment groups where >=2 judges voted and their verdicts disagree.\n\n    Groups that already carry an ``ai_consensus`` row are settled and never\n    returned.\n    \"\"\"\n    grouped, settled = _judge_rows_by_group(tool_id, base_dir)\n\n    splits: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for (run_id, finding_id, group_id), by_judge in sorted(grouped.items()):","numLines":200,"startLine":120,"totalLines":661}}}

Judge verdicts land DAYS after their\n1806\t                # cycle (the executor lane is nightly and drains a backlog),\n1807\t                # so the cycle filter permanently discarded every late\n1808\t                # verdict: zero ai_consensus rows ever existed. cycle_id=None\n1809\t                # is the engine's own process-all mode; the per-group"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"1d2639d9-7643-4f89-b4f1-4e0683ca652d","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:36:55.050Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/cycle.py","content":"    \"\"\"\n    from .convergence_drainer import _resolve_workspace_head_sha\n    from .feedback_store import generate_ai_consensus, generate_judgment_sample\n    from .genesis_policy import judgment_pipeline_policy\n    from .judge_fanout import (\n        dispatch_arbiter_for_anchor_groups,\n        dispatch_arbiter_for_split_verdicts,\n        dispatch_judges_for_sample,\n    )\n\n    target_sha = _resolve_workspace_head_sha(context.workspace_root)\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-786 — retire age-expired envelopes BEFORE minting, so the\n    # backlog cap below counts only envelopes still alive to claim. Expiry\n    # was lazy (discovered at claim time), the backlog read pending while\n    # being dead, and minting continued into the hole the drain could never\n    # fill within the TTL. Age-only sweep; repo-dependent refusals stay at\n    # the claim boundary.\n    from .agent_invocations import sweep_expired_anchors\n\n    anchor_sweep = sweep_expired_anchors(base_dir=context.base_dir)\n    # Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — sample size and backlog ceiling come from policy\n    # (the 5 was hardcoded; the ceiling did not exist and one week minted\n    # 462 envelopes against ~9 drained per night).\n    pipeline_policy = judgment_pipeline_policy(context.workspace_root)\n    sample_size = int(pipeline_policy.get(\"sample_size_per_tool\") or 5)\n    max_pending_per_role = int(pipeline_policy.get(\"max_pending_per_role\") or 32)\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-784 — judge weights for THIS cycle, computed in memory\n    # before the consensus that consumes them. The previous consumer read\n    # the calibration ledger's tail (`_cal_rows[-1]`), which the\n    # judge_calibration phase appends AFTER this phase runs — a permanent\n    # one-cycle lag, and None on any cycle with no prior row, which was\n    # every cycle (the only row ever written carried `judges: []`). The\n    # ledger append stays owned by the calibration phase for audit; the\n    # freshness source is the computation, not the tail. Failure semantics\n    # unchanged: any error → None → the legacy gate bit for bit; failure\n    # costs calibration, never consensus.\n    _judge_weights = None\n    try:\n        from .calibrated_intelligence import judge_weights_from_calibration\n        from .judge_calibration import score_judges\n\n        _judge_weights = judge_weights_from_calibration(\n            score_judges(cycle_id=context.cycle_id, base_dir=context.base_dir)\n        ) or None\n    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n        _judge_weights = None\n    sampled = 0\n    fanned_out = 0\n    mint_skipped_backlog = 0\n    consensus_rows = 0\n    arbiter_requests = 0\n    blocked: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for tool in list_tools(base_dir=context.base_dir):\n        tool_id = str(tool.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\")\n        if not tool_id:\n            continue\n        try:\n            sample = generate_judgment_sample(\n                tool_id=tool_id,\n                sample_size=sample_size,\n                strategy=\"stratified_by_uncertainty\",\n                cycle_id=context.cycle_id,\n                base_dir=context.base_dir,\n            )\n            sampled += len(sample.get(\"items\") or [])\n            fanout = dispatch_judges_for_sample(\n                sample=sample, base_dir=context.base_dir, target_sha=target_sha,\n                # E17-b — the same workspace target_sha was resolved from. The\n                # mint needs it to quote the cited lines into the envelope; a\n                # judge lane that dispatches two judges per finding is exactly\n                # where paying for the same file read twice is worst.\n                repo_root=context.workspace_root,\n                max_pending_per_role=max_pending_per_role,\n            )\n            fanned_out += len(fanout.get(\"minted\") or [])\n            mint_skipped_backlog += sum(\n                1 for s in fanout.get(\"skipped\") or []\n                if s.get(\"reason\") == \"mint_skipped_backlog\"\n            )\n        except GovernanceError as exc:\n            blocked.append({\"tool_id\": tool_id, \"step\": \"sample_or_fanout\", \"reason\": str(exc)[:200]})\n        try:\n            # Kalibre Zekâ Z2c — the conformal floor, derived per tool from\n            # correct-consensus confidences. Judge weights come from the\n            # in-memory computation above (ORPHAN-HIGH-784); the floor stays\n            # per-tool because its input is tool-scoped. Missing data → None\n            # → the legacy gate bit for bit; failure costs calibration,\n            # never consensus.\n            _conformal_floor = None\n            try:\n                from .calibrated_intelligence import conformal_threshold\n                from .feedback_store import load_feedback\n\n                _correct_confidences = [\n                    float(row.get(\"confidence\"))\n                    for row in load_feedback(tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=context.base_dir)\n                    if row.get(\"source_type\") == \"ai_consensus\"\n                    and isinstance(row.get(\"confidence\"), (int, float))\n                ]\n                _conformal_floor = conformal_threshold(_correct_confidences)\n            except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n                _conformal_floor = None\n            consensus = generate_ai_consensus(\n                tool_id=tool_id,\n                # D2 (Kapalı Döngü) — ledger-derived pending set, not the\n                # current cycle's runs. Judge verdicts land DAYS after their\n                # cycle (the executor lane is nightly and drains a backlog),\n                # so the cycle filter permanently discarded every late\n                # verdict: zero ai_consensus rows ever existed. cycle_id=None\n                # is the engine's own process-all mode; the per-group","numLines":110,"startLine":1700,"totalLines":3966}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01R5PxRdHVE4bMbUkr2DSau4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# FUXA Gap Implementation Plan\n2\t\n3\t## FUXA Dokümantasyon Analizi → Aquaculture Platform Eksik Modüller\n4\t\n5\t**Tarih:** 2026-03-17\n6\t**Kapsam:** FUXA'da olup bizim sistemde eksik olan tüm özellikler\n7\t**Mimari:** Enterprise-grade, React/NestJS, TypeScript-first\n8\t\n9\t---\n10\t\n11\t## GAP ÖZET MATRİSİ\n12\t\n13\t| # | Kategori | Mevcut | Eksik | Öncelik |\n14\t|---|----------|--------|-------|---------|\n15\t| 1 | Device/Protocol | %60 | WebAPI adapter, tag DAQ wiring, tag browser UI, device health check | KRİTİK |\n16\t| 2 | Script System | %90 | $getDevice, $invokeObject, scale scripts on tags | ORTA |\n17\t| 3 | Event System | %90 | Tag mapping placeholders, \"Set from Input\" action | DÜŞÜK |\n18\t| 4 | View System | %60 | @[tag_name] placeholders, Target Device nav, view lifecycle scripts | YÜKSEK |\n19\t| 5 | Widget System | %70 | Bar/Pie chart widget types, $invokeObject | ORTA |\n20\t| 6 | Scheduler | %85 | Timer/Event mode, script-on-start/end actions | DÜŞÜK |\n21\t| 7 | Chart System | %65 | CSV export wiring, PDF reports | ORTA |\n22\t| 8 | Alarm System | %90 | runScript action stub, retention config UI | ORTA |\n23\t| 9 | Security | %85 | API key management | DÜŞÜK |\n24\t| 10 | Project Mgmt | %80 | Auto-save, standalone JSON export/import | ORTA |\n25\t\n26\t**Not:** Node-RED entegrasyonu kasıtlı olarak atlandı — platformumuz IEC 61131-3 Structured Text kullanıyor.\n27\t\n28\t---\n29\t\n30\t## FAZ 1: Device/Protocol Altyapısı (KRİTİK)\n31\t\n32\t### 1.1 WebAPI Protocol Adapter (Backend)\n33\t\n34\t**Neden:** FUXA'da REST API polling ile cihaz verisi çekme desteği var. IoT gateway'lerden, 3rd party API'lerden veri çekmek için şart.\n35\t\n36\t**Dosyalar:**\n37\t- `apps/sensor-service/src/protocol/adapters/webapi-adapter.ts` — YENİ\n38\t- `apps/sensor-service/src/protocol/adapters/webapi-adapter.types.ts` — YENİ\n39\t\n40\t**Özellikler:**\n41\t- HTTP GET/POST polling (configurable interval: 1s-60s)\n42\t- JSON response parsing with JSONPath tag mapping\n43\t- Authentication: None, Basic, Bearer, API Key (header/query)\n44\t- SSL/TLS verification toggle\n45\t- Request headers configuration\n46\t- Response timeout (default 10s)\n47\t- Tag extraction: JSONPath expressions → tag values\n48\t- Error handling: retry with backoff, status code validation\n49\t- Device status: HTTP 2xx = online, timeout = offline, 4xx/5xx = error\n50\t\n51\t### 1.2 Tag DAQ Settings Wiring (Backend + Frontend)\n52\t\n53\t**Neden:** FUXA'da her tag'e ayrı ayrı DAQ ayarı (enabled, interval, deadband) verilebiliyor. Bizde tip tanımlı ama bağlı değil.\n54\t\n55\t**Dosyalar:**\n56\t- `apps/sensor-service/src/scada-runtime/services/tag-manager.service.ts` — GÜNCELLE\n57\t- `apps/sensor-service/src/scada-runtime/services/daq-storage.service.ts` — GÜNCELLE\n58\t- `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/tag-config/TagDaqSettingsPanel.tsx` — YENİ\n59\t\n60\t**Özellikler:**\n61\t- TagManagerService: DAQ settings per tag (enabled/interval/deadband)\n62\t- Deadband filtering: only store when |newValue - lastStored| > deadband\n63\t- Interval-based storage: store at configured interval regardless of change\n64\t- \"Changed\" mode: store only on value change (beyond deadband)\n65\t- \"Restored\" mode: reload last stored value on startup\n66\t- Frontend panel: toggle enabled, set interval (ms), set deadband, preview\n67\t\n68\t### 1.3 Generic Tag Browser Dialog (Frontend)\n69\t\n70\t**Neden:** FUXA'da her protokol için tag'leri browse edip seçebiliyorsun. Bizde OPC-UA node browse var ama generic UI yok.\n71\t\n72\t**Dosyalar:**\n73\t- `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/tag-config/TagBrowserDialog.tsx` — YENİ\n74\t- `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/tag-config/ProtocolTagTree.tsx` — YENİ\n75\t- `web/modules/sensor-module/src/hooks/useTagBrowser.ts` — YENİ\n76\t\n77\t**Özellikler:**\n78\t- Modal dialog with device selector dropdown\n79\t- Protocol-aware tag tree (OPC-UA: node hierarchy, Modbus: register ranges, MQTT: topic list)\n80\t- Search/filter within tags\n81\t- Tag preview (current value, type, quality)\n82\t- Multi-select and batch add\n83\t- Recent tags list\n84\t\n85\t### 1.4 Device Health Check Loop (Backend)\n86\t\n87\t**Neden:** FUXA 5-saniyede bir cihaz sağlık kontrolü yapıyor. Bizde WebSocket heartbeat var ama cihaz seviyesinde yok.\n88\t\n89\t**Dosyalar:**\n90\t- `apps/sensor-service/src/scada-runtime/services/device-health.service.ts` — YENİ\n91\t\n92\t**Özellikler:**\n93\t- 5-second polling loop per connected device\n94\t- Protocol-specific health check (OPC-UA: readServerStatus, Modbus: read holding register 0, MQTT: ping, WebAPI: HEAD request)\n95\t- Status transitions: online → warning (1 miss) → offline (3 miss)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"a9de9888-b8fa-41d7-a6ff-3b512e7ed887","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:39:32.326Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/docs/FUXA_GAP_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md","content":"# FUXA Gap Implementation Plan\n\n## FUXA Dokümantasyon Analizi → Aquaculture Platform Eksik Modüller\n\n**Tarih:** 2026-03-17\n**Kapsam:** FUXA'da olup bizim sistemde eksik olan tüm özellikler\n**Mimari:** Enterprise-grade, React/NestJS, TypeScript-first\n\n---\n\n## GAP ÖZET MATRİSİ\n\n| # | Kategori | Mevcut | Eksik | Öncelik |\n|---|----------|--------|-------|---------|\n| 1 | Device/Protocol | %60 | WebAPI adapter, tag DAQ wiring, tag browser UI, device health check | KRİTİK |\n| 2 | Script System | %90 | $getDevice, $invokeObject, scale scripts on tags | ORTA |\n| 3 | Event System | %90 | Tag mapping placeholders, \"Set from Input\" action | DÜŞÜK |\n| 4 | View System | %60 | @[tag_name] placeholders, Target Device nav, view lifecycle scripts | YÜKSEK |\n| 5 | Widget System | %70 | Bar/Pie chart widget types, $invokeObject | ORTA |\n| 6 | Scheduler | %85 | Timer/Event mode, script-on-start/end actions | DÜŞÜK |\n| 7 | Chart System | %65 | CSV export wiring, PDF reports | ORTA |\n| 8 | Alarm System | %90 | runScript action stub, retention config UI | ORTA |\n| 9 | Security | %85 | API key management | DÜŞÜK |\n| 10 | Project Mgmt | %80 | Auto-save, standalone JSON export/import | ORTA |\n\n**Not:** Node-RED entegrasyonu kasıtlı olarak atlandı — platformumuz IEC 61131-3 Structured Text kullanıyor.\n\n---\n\n## FAZ 1: Device/Protocol Altyapısı (KRİTİK)\n\n### 1.1 WebAPI Protocol Adapter (Backend)\n\n**Neden:** FUXA'da REST API polling ile cihaz verisi çekme desteği var. IoT gateway'lerden, 3rd party API'lerden veri çekmek için şart.\n\n**Dosyalar:**\n- `apps/sensor-service/src/protocol/adapters/webapi-adapter.ts` — YENİ\n- `apps/sensor-service/src/protocol/adapters/webapi-adapter.types.ts` — YENİ\n\n**Özellikler:**\n- HTTP GET/POST polling (configurable interval: 1s-60s)\n- JSON response parsing with JSONPath tag mapping\n- Authentication: None, Basic, Bearer, API Key (header/query)\n- SSL/TLS verification toggle\n- Request headers configuration\n- Response timeout (default 10s)\n- Tag extraction: JSONPath expressions → tag values\n- Error handling: retry with backoff, status code validation\n- Device status: HTTP 2xx = online, timeout = offline, 4xx/5xx = error\n\n### 1.2 Tag DAQ Settings Wiring (Backend + Frontend)\n\n**Neden:** FUXA'da her tag'e ayrı ayrı DAQ ayarı (enabled, interval, deadband) verilebiliyor. Bizde tip tanımlı ama bağlı değil.\n\n**Dosyalar:**\n- `apps/sensor-service/src/scada-runtime/services/tag-manager.service.ts` — GÜNCELLE\n- `apps/sensor-service/src/scada-runtime/services/daq-storage.service.ts` — GÜNCELLE\n- `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/tag-config/TagDaqSettingsPanel.tsx` — YENİ\n\n**Özellikler:**\n- TagManagerService: DAQ settings per tag (enabled/interval/deadband)\n- Deadband filtering: only store when |newValue - lastStored| > deadband\n- Interval-based storage: store at configured interval regardless of change\n- \"Changed\" mode: store only on value change (beyond deadband)\n- \"Restored\" mode: reload last stored value on startup\n- Frontend panel: toggle enabled, set interval (ms), set deadband, preview\n\n### 1.3 Generic Tag Browser Dialog (Frontend)\n\n**Neden:** FUXA'da her protokol için tag'leri browse edip seçebiliyorsun. Bizde OPC-UA node browse var ama generic UI yok.\n\n**Dosyalar:**\n- `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/tag-config/TagBrowserDialog.tsx` — YENİ\n- `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/tag-config/ProtocolTagTree.tsx` — YENİ\n- `web/modules/sensor-module/src/hooks/useTagBrowser.ts` — YENİ\n\n**Özellikler:**\n- Modal dialog with device selector dropdown\n- Protocol-aware tag tree (OPC-UA: node hierarchy, Modbus: register ranges, MQTT: topic list)\n- Search/filter within tags\n- Tag preview (current value, type, quality)\n- Multi-select and batch add\n- Recent tags list\n\n### 1.4 Device Health Check Loop (Backend)\n\n**Neden:** FUXA 5-saniyede bir cihaz sağlık kontrolü yapıyor. Bizde WebSocket heartbeat var ama cihaz seviyesinde yok.\n\n**Dosyalar:**\n- `apps/sensor-service/src/scada-runtime/services/device-health.service.ts` — YENİ\n\n**Özellikler:**\n- 5-second polling loop per connected device\n- Protocol-specific health check (OPC-UA: readServerStatus, Modbus: read holding register 0, MQTT: ping, WebAPI: HEAD request)\n- Status transitions: online → warning (1 miss) → offline (3 miss)","numLines":95,"startLine":1,"totalLines":391}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011sWxYiTY1URCBtUejisdKf","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# SCADA Package Builder - Entegrasyon Dokumantasyonu\n2\t\n3\tBu dokuman, SCADA Package Builder'in placeholder durumdan tam calisir hale getirilmesi icin yapilan degisiklikleri detayli olarak aciklar.\n4\t\n5\t---\n6\t\n7\t## 1. Genel Bakis\n8\t\n9\tSCADA Builder UI bilesenleri (WidgetPalette, ScreenCanvas, PropertiesPanel, DeployScadaDialog, widget-configs) onceden implement edilmisti ancak birbirine bagli degildi. Bu calisma ile:\n10\t\n11\t- **BuilderPage** tum bilesenleri birbirine bagladi\n12\t- **Save/Load** gercek GraphQL mutation'lariyla calisiyor\n13\t- **Deploy** dialog entegre edildi\n14\t- **Tag Browser** sistemi eklendi (edge device I/O tag'lerini browse etme)\n15\t- **Hedef Cihaz Secici** toolbar'a eklendi\n16\t- **ScreenTabBar** component'i kullanilmaya baslandi\n17\t\n18\t---\n19\t\n20\t## 2. Yeni Dosyalar\n21\t\n22\t### 2.1 TagBrowser.tsx\n23\t**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/TagBrowser.tsx`\n24\t\n25\tEdge device'in I/O konfigurasyonundan tag listesi gosteren searchable dropdown/autocomplete bileseni.\n26\t\n27\t**Props:**\n28\t```typescript\n29\tinterface TagBrowserProps {\n30\t  deviceId: string | null;    // Hedef edge device ID\n31\t  value: string;              // Secili tag adi\n32\t  onChange: (tagName: string) => void;\n33\t  placeholder?: string;\n34\t  multiple?: boolean;         // Coklu secim (TrendChart icin)\n35\t}\n36\t```\n37\t\n38\t**Ozellikler:**\n39\t- Tag'leri I/O tipine gore gruplar: Analog Input (AI), Analog Output (AO), Digital Input (DI), Digital Output (DO)\n40\t- Her tag icin IO badge'i, tag adi, birim ve kanal numarasi gosterir\n41\t- Arama/filtre destegi\n42\t- Manuel metin girisi destegi (listede olmayan tag'ler icin)\n43\t- Multiple modda chip'ler ile coklu secim\n44\t- Empty state'ler: cihaz secilmemis, tag bulunamadi, yukleniyor\n45\t\n46\t### 2.2 useDeviceTags.ts\n47\t**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/hooks/useDeviceTags.ts`\n48\t\n49\tEdge device'tan tag listesi cekmek icin hook.\n50\t\n51\t```typescript\n52\tfunction useDeviceTags(deviceId: string | null): {\n53\t  tags: TagInfo[];\n54\t  groupedTags: TagGroup[];\n55\t  loading: boolean;\n56\t  error: string | null;\n57\t}\n58\t```\n59\t\n60\t- Mevcut `useEdgeDevice` hook'unu kullanir\n61\t- `DeviceIoConfig` entity'sindeki `ioConfig` dizisinden aktif tag'leri parse eder\n62\t- Tag'leri AI > AO > DI > DO sirasinda gruplar\n63\t\n64\t### 2.3 device-tags.queries.ts\n65\t**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/graphql/device-tags.queries.ts`\n66\t\n67\tEdge device I/O tag'leri icin odaklanmis GraphQL query. `edgeDevice(id)` query'sinin sadece `ioConfig` field'ini isteyen versiyonu.\n68\t\n69\t---\n70\t\n71\t## 3. Degisiklik Yapilan Dosyalar\n72\t\n73\t### 3.1 ScadaPackageBuilderPage.tsx (Tam Yeniden Yazim)\n74\t**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/pages/scada/ScadaPackageBuilderPage.tsx`\n75\t\n76\t**Onceki durum:** Placeholder div'ler, no-op save/load handler'lari, deploy baglantisi yok.\n77\t\n78\t**Yeni durum:**\n79\t\n80\t#### Save/Load Entegrasyonu\n81\t- `useCreateScadaPackage` ve `useUpdateScadaPackage` mutation hook'lari kullaniliyor\n82\t- `packageId` varsa update, yoksa create cagrilir\n83\t- Basarili create sonrasi URL `/sensor/scada-builder/${newId}` olarak guncellenir (`useNavigate`)\n84\t- Basari toast'u (2 sn yesil badge)\n85\t- Mevcut paket yuklenirken loading spinner gosterilir\n86\t- `useScadaPackageById` ile route param'dan paket yuklenir\n87\t\n88\t#### PropertiesPanel Entegrasyonu\n89\t- Placeholder div yerine gercek `<PropertiesPanel>` component'i render ediliyor\n90\t- Store'dan `selectedWidgetId` ile aktif widget bulunuyor\n91\t- `onWidgetConfigChange` → `updateWidget(screenId, widgetId, { config: {...existing, ...updates} })`\n92\t- `alarmRules`, `controlPermissions`, `trendConfig` store'dan okunup PropertiesPanel'e aktariliyor\n93\t- `deviceId` prop'u PropertiesPanel'e iletiliyor (widget config'lerdeki TagBrowser icin)\n94\t\n95\t#### Deploy Entegrasyonu\n96\t- \"Edge Device'a Deploy\" butonu `DeployScadaDialog`'u acar\n97\t- Deploy oncesi kaydedilmemis degisiklik varsa otomatik save yapar\n98\t- `packageId`, `packageName`, `packageData` (toScadaPackageJSON) dialog'a aktarilir\n99\t\n100\t#### Hedef Cihaz Secici (Toolbar)\n101\t- Paket adi yaninda device selector dropdown\n102\t- `useEdgeDevices` hook'u ile cihaz listesi\n103\t- Online/offline badge gosterimi\n104\t- Secilen device → store'daki `targetDeviceId`'ye yazilir\n105\t- Status bar'da secili cihaz bilgisi\n106\t\n107\t#### ScreenTabBar Entegrasyonu\n108\t- Inline tab kodu kaldirildi, `<ScreenTabBar>` component'i kullaniliyor\n109\t- Tip secimli ekran ekleme (dashboard/process/alarms/trends/calibration/control)\n110\t- Sag-tik context menu: Yeniden Adlandir, Cokla, Varsayilan Yap, Sil\n111\t\n112\t### 3.2 scadaPackageStore.ts\n113\t**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/store/scadaPackageStore.ts`\n114\t\n115\t**Eklenen state ve action'lar:**\n116\t- `targetDeviceId: string | null` — Secili edge device ID\n117\t- `setTargetDeviceId(id)` — Device ID guncelleme action'i\n118\t- `toScadaPackageJSON()` → `meta.edgeDeviceId` alani eklendi\n119\t- `loadFromJSON()` → `json.meta.edgeDeviceId` okunuyor\n120\t\n121\t### 3.3 PropertiesPanel.tsx\n122\t**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/PropertiesPanel.tsx`\n123\t\n124\t**Degisiklikler:**\n125\t- `PropertiesPanelProps` interface'ine `deviceId?: string | null` eklendi\n126\t- Component props'unda `deviceId` destructure ediliyor\n127\t- `<ConfigComponent>` render'inda `deviceId={deviceId}` prop'u geciriliyor\n128\t- Bu sayede widget config'lerdeki TagBrowser hedef cihazin tag'lerini gorebiliyor\n129\t\n130\t### 3.4 widget-configs/index.ts\n131\t**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/widget-configs/index.ts`\n132\t\n133\t- `WidgetConfigProps` SCADA Package Builder - Entegrasyon Dokumantasyonu\n\nBu dokuman, SCADA Package Builder'in placeholder durumdan tam calisir hale getirilmesi icin yapilan degisiklikleri detayli olarak aciklar.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Genel Bakis\n\nSCADA Builder UI bilesenleri (WidgetPalette, ScreenCanvas, PropertiesPanel, DeployScadaDialog, widget-configs) onceden implement edilmisti ancak birbirine bagli degildi. Bu calisma ile:\n\n- **BuilderPage** tum bilesenleri birbirine bagladi\n- **Save/Load** gercek GraphQL mutation'lariyla calisiyor\n- **Deploy** dialog entegre edildi\n- **Tag Browser** sistemi eklendi (edge device I/O tag'lerini browse etme)\n- **Hedef Cihaz Secici** toolbar'a eklendi\n- **ScreenTabBar** component'i kullanilmaya baslandi\n\n---\n\n## 2. Yeni Dosyalar\n\n### 2.1 TagBrowser.tsx\n**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/TagBrowser.tsx`\n\nEdge device'in I/O konfigurasyonundan tag listesi gosteren searchable dropdown/autocomplete bileseni.\n\n**Props:**\n```typescript\ninterface TagBrowserProps {\n  deviceId: string | null;    // Hedef edge device ID\n  value: string;              // Secili tag adi\n  onChange: (tagName: string) => void;\n  placeholder?: string;\n  multiple?: boolean;         // Coklu secim (TrendChart icin)\n}\n```\n\n**Ozellikler:**\n- Tag'leri I/O tipine gore gruplar: Analog Input (AI), Analog Output (AO), Digital Input (DI), Digital Output (DO)\n- Her tag icin IO badge'i, tag adi, birim ve kanal numarasi gosterir\n- Arama/filtre destegi\n- Manuel metin girisi destegi (listede olmayan tag'ler icin)\n- Multiple modda chip'ler ile coklu secim\n- Empty state'ler: cihaz secilmemis, tag bulunamadi, yukleniyor\n\n### 2.2 useDeviceTags.ts\n**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/hooks/useDeviceTags.ts`\n\nEdge device'tan tag listesi cekmek icin hook.\n\n```typescript\nfunction useDeviceTags(deviceId: string | null): {\n  tags: TagInfo[];\n  groupedTags: TagGroup[];\n  loading: boolean;\n  error: string | null;\n}\n```\n\n- Mevcut `useEdgeDevice` hook'unu kullanir\n- `DeviceIoConfig` entity'sindeki `ioConfig` dizisinden aktif tag'leri parse eder\n- Tag'leri AI > AO > DI > DO sirasinda gruplar\n\n### 2.3 device-tags.queries.ts\n**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/graphql/device-tags.queries.ts`\n\nEdge device I/O tag'leri icin odaklanmis GraphQL query. `edgeDevice(id)` query'sinin sadece `ioConfig` field'ini isteyen versiyonu.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Degisiklik Yapilan Dosyalar\n\n### 3.1 ScadaPackageBuilderPage.tsx (Tam Yeniden Yazim)\n**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/pages/scada/ScadaPackageBuilderPage.tsx`\n\n**Onceki durum:** Placeholder div'ler, no-op save/load handler'lari, deploy baglantisi yok.\n\n**Yeni durum:**\n\n#### Save/Load Entegrasyonu\n- `useCreateScadaPackage` ve `useUpdateScadaPackage` mutation hook'lari kullaniliyor\n- `packageId` varsa update, yoksa create cagrilir\n- Basarili create sonrasi URL `/sensor/scada-builder/${newId}` olarak guncellenir (`useNavigate`)\n- Basari toast'u (2 sn yesil badge)\n- Mevcut paket yuklenirken loading spinner gosterilir\n- `useScadaPackageById` ile route param'dan paket yuklenir\n\n#### PropertiesPanel Entegrasyonu\n- Placeholder div yerine gercek `<PropertiesPanel>` component'i render ediliyor\n- Store'dan `selectedWidgetId` ile aktif widget bulunuyor\n- `onWidgetConfigChange` → `updateWidget(screenId, widgetId, { config: {...existing, ...updates} })`\n- `alarmRules`, `controlPermissions`, `trendConfig` store'dan okunup PropertiesPanel'e aktariliyor\n- `deviceId` prop'u PropertiesPanel'e iletiliyor (widget config'lerdeki TagBrowser icin)\n\n#### Deploy Entegrasyonu\n- \"Edge Device'a Deploy\" butonu `DeployScadaDialog`'u acar\n- Deploy oncesi kaydedilmemis degisiklik varsa otomatik save yapar\n- `packageId`, `packageName`, `packageData` (toScadaPackageJSON) dialog'a aktarilir\n\n#### Hedef Cihaz Secici (Toolbar)\n- Paket adi yaninda device selector dropdown\n- `useEdgeDevices` hook'u ile cihaz listesi\n- Online/offline badge gosterimi\n- Secilen device → store'daki `targetDeviceId`'ye yazilir\n- Status bar'da secili cihaz bilgisi\n\n#### ScreenTabBar Entegrasyonu\n- Inline tab kodu kaldirildi, `<ScreenTabBar>` component'i kullaniliyor\n- Tip secimli ekran ekleme (dashboard/process/alarms/trends/calibration/control)\n- Sag-tik context menu: Yeniden Adlandir, Cokla, Varsayilan Yap, Sil\n\n### 3.2 scadaPackageStore.ts\n**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/store/scadaPackageStore.ts`\n\n**Eklenen state ve action'lar:**\n- `targetDeviceId: string | null` — Secili edge device ID\n- `setTargetDeviceId(id)` — Device ID guncelleme action'i\n- `toScadaPackageJSON()` → `meta.edgeDeviceId` alani eklendi\n- `loadFromJSON()` → `json.meta.edgeDeviceId` okunuyor\n\n### 3.3 PropertiesPanel.tsx\n**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/PropertiesPanel.tsx`\n\n**Degisiklikler:**\n- `PropertiesPanelProps` interface'ine `deviceId?: string | null` eklendi\n- Component props'unda `deviceId` destructure ediliyor\n- `<ConfigComponent>` render'inda `deviceId={deviceId}` prop'u geciriliyor\n- Bu sayede widget config'lerdeki TagBrowser hedef cihazin tag'lerini gorebiliyor\n\n### 3.4 widget-configs/index.ts\n**Konum:** `web/modules/sensor-module/src/components/scada-builder/widget-configs/index.ts`\n\n- `WidgetConfigProps` interface'ine `deviceId?: string | null` eklendi\n- Tum widget config component'leri bu yeni prop'u aliyor\n","numLines":135,"startLine":1,"totalLines":212}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Fmx8u7s7ChzsLMcXHMQm4v","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# HMI Dönüşüm Planı: Simulation → Operatör HMI\n2\t\n3\t## FUXA Analizi + Aquaculture Platform Mevcut Durum → Hedef Mimari\n4\t\n5\t**Tarih:** 2026-03-17\n6\t**Kapsam:** FUXA'daki tüm HMI/SCADA yeteneklerini React/NestJS tabanlı aquaculture platform'a entegre etmek\n7\t\n8\t---\n9\t\n10\t## 1. MEVCUT DURUM ANALİZİ\n11\t\n12\t### 1.1 Aquaculture Platform (Bugün)\n13\t\n14\t**SCADA Builder (Editör Modu):**\n15\t- ReactFlow tabanlı canvas, 27 widget türü\n16\t- Grid-based layout, drag-and-drop, snap\n17\t- Widget renderers: gauge, numericDisplay, statusIndicator, tankLevel, trendChart, toggleSwitch, slider, numericInput, pushButton, emergencyStop, alarmBanner, alarmList, equipment, feeder, radialFilter, cleanWaterTank, dirtyWaterTank, mbbr, hepaFilter, calibrationWizard/History/Status, screenLink, staticText, processView\n18\t- 36+ equipment symbols (pumps, valves, tanks, heat exchangers)\n19\t- 3 edge type: orthogonal, multiHandle, draggable\n20\t- 9 connection type (ISA-5.1 P&ID: process-pipe, electrical, pneumatic, hydraulic, instrument, data-link, capillary, steam, drain-vent)\n21\t- Multi-screen support (6 screen type)\n22\t- Alarm rules with deadband/delay\n23\t- ST language parser + interpreter (simulation)\n24\t- Tag binding via useDeviceTags hook\n25\t- Undo/redo, copy-paste\n26\t\n27\t**SCADA Viewer (Runtime Modu):**\n28\t- ScadaViewer.tsx — iframe-based read-only display\n29\t- PostMessage communication\n30\t- 5 runtime widget (NumericWidget, GaugeWidget, SparklineWidget, StatusWidget, WidgetContainer)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"36148bec-0d82-47e8-b0dd-b970b025de87","uuid":"876cf9dd-84e2-45f9-a193-1dd4f7695dda","timestamp":"2026-09-20T12:41:44.474Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-310a6c39c90e/docs/HMI_TRANSFORMATION_PLAN.md","content":"# HMI Dönüşüm Planı: Simulation → Operatör HMI\n\n## FUXA Analizi + Aquaculture Platform Mevcut Durum → Hedef Mimari\n\n**Tarih:** 2026-03-17\n**Kapsam:** FUXA'daki tüm HMI/SCADA yeteneklerini React/NestJS tabanlı aquaculture platform'a entegre etmek\n\n---\n\n## 1. MEVCUT DURUM ANALİZİ\n\n### 1.1 Aquaculture Platform (Bugün)\n\n**SCADA Builder (Editör Modu):**\n- ReactFlow tabanlı canvas, 27 widget türü\n- Grid-based layout, drag-and-drop, snap\n- Widget renderers: gauge, numericDisplay, statusIndicator, tankLevel, trendChart, toggleSwitch, slider, numericInput, pushButton, emergencyStop, alarmBanner, alarmList, equipment, feeder, radialFilter, cleanWaterTank, dirtyWaterTank, mbbr, hepaFilter, calibrationWizard/History/Status, screenLink, staticText, processView\n- 36+ equipment symbols (pumps, valves, tanks, heat exchangers)\n- 3 edge type: orthogonal, multiHandle, draggable\n- 9 connection type (ISA-5.1 P&ID: process-pipe, electrical, pneumatic, hydraulic, instrument, data-link, capillary, steam, drain-vent)\n- Multi-screen support (6 screen type)\n- Alarm rules with deadband/delay\n- ST language parser + interpreter (simulation)\n- Tag binding via useDeviceTags hook\n- Undo/redo, copy-paste\n\n**SCADA Viewer (Runtime Modu):**\n- ScadaViewer.tsx — iframe-based read-only display\n- PostMessage communication\n- 5 runtime widget (NumericWidget, GaugeWidget, SparklineWidget, StatusWidget, WidgetContainer)","numLines":30,"startLine":1,"totalLines":979}}}
